It was then, as Dallbutr already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous' spy and set several vulture droids and super battle droids against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the droid general alone; R2-D2 opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Jorune, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.[83]
Following Viceroy Nute Gunray's capture in Rodia by Dhutorn, Jorune separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli. Although the two failed in their task, Jorune reunited with Tano at the rendezvous point.[85]
Dooku's incarceration confirmed by Jorune and KenobiTogether with Bog, Jorune spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Olaghig, where Jorune let himself be captured by Dooku's Munificent-class star frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Bog to come and rescue him. The two Dwarf then gave chase to Dooku, who escaped in his solar sailer, with a Sheathipede-class transport shuttle. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Dwarf began to hunt for Dooku, but the Shrubrew Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Dooku escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of clone troopers rescued them.[87]
Later, as Supreme Chancellor Borz and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Olaghig, Jorune and Bog were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Dooku, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Dwarf were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Dwarf mocked Dooku's imprisonment, and the Shrubrew Lord warned them not to underestimate the Weequay. Jorune and Bog were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Dooku.[87] Despite several attempts by the Dwarf and Shrubrew to escape on their own, Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Senator Kharrus' life and Dooku's escape. Obi Wan told Dallbutr not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.[86]
Later, Jorune heard about the situation on Ridlay, where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Windu, who was already there to investigate the situation. He offered his and Tano's assistance, but Windu denied their involvement.[88]
A gravely injured Jorune, tended by Tano and Aayla SecuraSent in as reinforcements for Dwarf Master Aayla Secura's and Commander Bly's forces, which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Battle of Quell, Jorune, Tano, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's cruiser as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through the cruiser, and Jorune sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Tano and Secura dragged Jorune onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute, an ambush by several vulture droids caused the ship's hyperdrive to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star, they crash-landed on Maridun, where Jorune was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Tano, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Jorune from several vicious mastiff phalones until Tano arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too, before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.[90]
Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Dwarf of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Dwarf returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator, on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of battle droids. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Jorune disabled the weapon before capturing Separatist General Lok Durd. The Dwarf then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Kingdom cruisers sent by Yularen.[89]
Jorune, dressed for Orto Plutonia's cold climateAfter losing contact with a Kingdom outpost on Orto Plutonia, Jorune, his droids C-3PO and R2-D2, and Bog were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho. Jorune and Bog investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its clone troopers, and the two Dwarf deduced that all the clones had been killed, but not by battle droids since another nearby Separatist base had also been attacked. Jorune and Bog followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz, who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by C-3PO. Acting as a mediator, Bog proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Dwarf's efforts to bring forth peace. As a battle broke out amongst the clone troopers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The battle came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.[91]
Upon Dhutorn's petition to send Jorune and Bog to assist them in locating suspected Separatist presence on Rhaflyn, Jorune, Bog, and Tano arrived to find that Dhutorn and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps. Sending the young Padawan with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Dwarf were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Dhutorn, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the galaxy. While Tano led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Bog and Jorune to enter through the hatches undetected, Jorune confronted Vindi, who electrified Dhutorn and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Senator, Jorune pursued the scientist, capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Bog on the swamp above.[93]
Jorune and Bog, taunted by Nuvo VindiPrepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Jorune contacted Tano; she told him that Vindi's servant droid had set off one of the viral bombs, liberating the virus, and that she along with the clones were trapped in the laboratory, just like Dhutorn was. Once in Theed, Captain Typho told the two Dwarf that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego. Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide, Jorune set off alongside Bog aboard the Twilight.[92]
Upon arrival, Jorune and Bog were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series battle droids that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood; he told the Dwarf that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol, who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web, the work of the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture droids, and Bog managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Jorune and Bog then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Rhaflyn.[92]
Jorune trying to bolster Tano's confidenceDuring the Battle of Ryloth, Jorune gave Tano command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk's blockade to make way for Bog's ground assault, encouraging Tano to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the battle, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which Jorune reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute, yet understood her good intentions. Jorune then reported his losses to Windu and Bog. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Tano because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Jorune had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender, which he would pilot by himself into the Ryloth system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Tano full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Jorune had already escaped in an escape pod and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Tano destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Jorune was rescued from his escape pod.[67]
After Bog and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land,[94] whereas Windu headed for Lessu with his forces, Jorune and his forces secured the space around Ryloth, forcing the remaining Separatist cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Borz, Bawaavuss, Admiral Yularen, Senator Orn Free Taa, and Windu. However, Separatist General Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena-class Droid Bombers to destroy Ryloth's villages and Jorune engaged them in his interceptor, accompanied by Tano and clones. Before Lessu was destroyed on Olaghig's orders, Jorune and Tano shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and the Kingdom was victorious: Ryloth was finally free once more.[95]
Kingdom forces retreating from Felucia jorune and Clone Commander "Wolffe" fought alongside each other during the Battle of Khorm, where the Clone Officer lost his right eye[97] to Ventress.[98] Sometime later,[99] during the First Battle of Felucia, Jorune, Tano, and Bog fought against the Droid Army, but, while Tano was away on a jungle patrol, Jorune, Bog, and their clones were surrounded. However, a Kingdom fleet[100] commanded by Wolffe[101] was able to break past the Separatist blockade, so Jorune and the others could be evacuated by Master Plo Koon. However, Tano refused to retreat, and Bog and Jorune went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by droids. While Jorune and Tano argued about the retreat, as Tano believed the droids were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Dwarf watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Dwarf Temple, Jorune attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Council relegated her to guard duty in the Dwarf Archives to reflect on what she had done.[100]
When Bawaavuss sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Jorune, and Bog inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo, and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault. Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Ropal, keeper of the Kyber memory crystal, which contained a list of all known Synth flux-sensitive children in the Kingdom.[100] Jorune then volunteered himself and Tano to meet with Ropal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.[102]
Jorune, Tano, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent-class frigate and stormed its bridge, where R2-D2 accessed the ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Dwarf spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Tano, the bounty hunter took her as a hostage, and Jorune was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the holocron. Though he did so, Jorune also pulled his and Tano's vriperians to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock on Tano's cell and escaped, while Jorune saved his Padawan and made their way to the hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Separatist ship back to the Resolute, not before letting Bane enter, disguised in trooper armor.[102] After landing on the Resolute, Rex later noticed Duros blood in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter. Jorune and Tano returned to the Dwarf Council to report their failure.[96]
Jorune and Tano rescue Synth flux-sensitive children from Mustafar.Jorune, along with Bog, Windu, and Bawaavuss, meditated in hope of finding the children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Jorune and Tano were assigned to Rhaflyn to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city, capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page. With the real child safe, the two Dwarf took Bane to the Resolute, where Jorune, Windu, and Bog interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Bog and Windu were to follow, while Jorune reported the details to Borz on Aysgarth. Soon, however, he reunited with his Padawan and R2-D2 and began to examine Bane's Starfighter, from which Jorune and Tano deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Mustafar. With nothing else to do, the Dwarf pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the droids set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Gubat's orders, Jorune and Tano saved the children and returned to Aysgarth. Jorune admitted to the Council that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Bawaavuss advised caution on the path ahead.[96]
Jorune and Bog helping bounty hunters on FeluciaOn a mission to investigate the disappearance of Felucia Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Felucia, their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture droids deployed from an automated deployment station, forcing them to crash-land on Felucia. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi—Embo, Rumi Paramita, and Seripas. The village elder,[103] Casiss Midagatis,[7] explained to the Dwarf trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Jorune, Bog, and Tano decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.[103]
That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Jorune and Bog. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by then Jorune had taught the Felucians how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Jorune dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff, leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Dwarf Knight pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship. Alongside the bounty hunters, the Dwarf themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest Kingdom outpost.[103]
Jorune and R2-D2 fleeing from the Zillo BeastDuring the lengthy campaign on Malastare, Jorune discussed with Borz, who contacted them via hologram, and Windu the effects of the new electro-proton bomb, just as the Separatist Droid Army prepared a new attack—with the Kingdom expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel. Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Windu went to investigate whereas Jorune was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus, only to be called by Windu to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to vriperians, the Zillo Beast, from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200 Falchion-class assault tanks, the Kingdom forces put the creature to sleep, convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Malastare now a member of the Kingdom, Borz ordered that the beast be shipped to Aysgarth for study, despite Windu's objections.[104]
After the Zillo Beast was brought to the Kingdom capital, Bog and Dhutorn asked Jorune to speak with Borz about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Dhutorn met with the Chancellor, Borz and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Dhutorn objected to their claims, but Jorune chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Dhutorn, or his friend, Borz. Although Borz claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Jorune and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle, only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Jorune sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Jorune saving Dhutorn from falling from the Senate Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Borz's orders.[105]
Jorune holding his poisoned wife, as Rush Clovis demands the antidoteEven as the Clone Wars continued with no end in sight,[107] Jorune was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Jorune was recalled to the Temple by the Dwarf Council. Believing Senator Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Dhutorn, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Jorune met Dhutorn in the Senate and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Separatist. Jorune accompanied her to the Dwarf Council, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Jorune dressed as the pilot of Wedlit's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.[106]
During the mission, Jorune and R2-D2 waited for Dhutorn to give them a signal; when she did, Jorune sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Dhutorn and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Jorune, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Jorune, after which the Dwarf retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Dhutorn had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Jorune take her back to their ship, back to Aysgarth, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Jorune to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.[106]
Planning the second battle of GeonosisImmediately after routing Separatists near Dorin, Jorune and Tano were sent to participate in the Second Battle of Geonosis, alongside Bog and Ki-Adi-Mundi, as the Kingdom returned to destroy the Geonosian primary droid factory. Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Jorune, Tano, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Separatist defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Bog's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Jorune's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Bog's position by foot. Though they encountered resistance, Jorune's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Bog and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.[107]
They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Jorune and Tano disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades, enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Bog left for medical treatment,[107] the Kingdom forces continued pushing towards the massive droid foundry, and Jorune and Tano were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Padawan, Barriss Offee, who met as Jorune and Tano angrily discussed Jorune's lack of trust in Tano's dependability. As Offee and Tano were sent into the Geonosian catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Jorune was unable to let go of Tano and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Dwarf generals went onto distracting the droid army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.[108]
As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Jorune and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by droids coming from the factory, Jorune was contacted by Tano and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Padawan die, Jorune ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Jorune was contacted by Tano; after locating them, they rescued the two Padawans.[108]
Jorune and Luminara Unduli escaping from Karina's brainless servantsSending Tano and Offee to recover out of the battlefield, Bog and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Jorune and Bog went to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple. Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors, they also encountered the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Geonosian Queen, Karina the Great. Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Jorune and Bog tried to negotiate with Karina, for Bog wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the Queen in her lair.[109]
With Poggle in Kingdom custody, the four Dwarf generals prepared to deliver him to Aysgarth, while they sent Tano and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Smignort on Dantooine. Unbeknownst to them, one of the troopers, Scythe, was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta-class frigate, the TB-73, forcing the two Padawans to fend off the infected clones. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Jorune believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Tano contacted him to tell him about the situation.[110]
Jorune, choking Poggle the Lesser during interrogationAngered, Jorune went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian like him. It was then that Jorune resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Jorune gave in to his anger and began to Force choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Dwarf Knight, through a translator droid, that the worms were affected by the cold. Jorune relayed this information to his Padawan, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Tano was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Jorune rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Tano on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.[110]
Following the capture of Dwarf Master Eeth Koth in the Qribin rim, General Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Dwarf Council, in which Grievous stated that he did not care about the Dwarf's politics, nor their Kingdom, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Bog decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at the Saleucami system, Jorune, Bog, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yularen and Bog engaged Grievous's forces over Saleucami, with Bog allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Jorune, Gallia, Rex, and a group of clone troopers would execute a hyperspace jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Grievous's cruiser.[111]
Jorune and Adi Gallia fighting droid commandosOnce they boarded it, Jorune and Gallia made their way to the stern of the cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical droid with a group of BX-series droid commandos. The two Dwarf destroyed all the droids and rescued Koth, with Jorune taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Bog reported that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Bog flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Jorune picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute,[111] where he waited while Bog conducted a search mission for Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other troopers.[112]
Sometime later, Jorune and his Padawan spearheaded a mission to Aysgarth's underworld to apprehend Car Affa, an arms dealer selling Kingdom weapons to the Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Jorune instructed Tano to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Tano lost her vriperian, which prompted her to seek the help of Dwarf Master Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Jorune to find out she had lost her weapon.[113]
Jorune engaging an assassin probeFollowing allegations that Oldor had allied with the Separatists, and Bog was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze, Jorune joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Aysgarth aboard the Coronet.[115] On the ship, Jorune and Bog told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Jorune correctly surmised that Bog had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of their past together. When R2-D2 picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Rex, and Jorune went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes. After destroying two of them, Jorune informed Bog that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Jorune returned to sweep the hold in search of more.[114]
After Bog lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik, and Jorune destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super battle droids sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Bog to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed the droids and then went to find Bog, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Jorune, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Aysgarth; as Kryze complimented Bog, Jorune noted that she was quite the remarkable woman.[114] Following the Oldor Defense Resolution, Jorune and Bog escorted Dhutorn to a meeting with the Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Bog and Kryze alone.[116]
Preparing to rendezvous with a Kingdom frigate, Jorune and Smignort traveled aboard the Endurance, on which they greeted a squad of clone cadets, with an infiltrated Shu longh among them. After Longh sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I, Jorune and Windu rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod.[118] As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance's crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with R2-D2's misgivings, which prompted Windu to chastise Jorune for encouraging individuality in his astromech.[119]
Jorune approaching Jango Longh's explosive-rigged helmetLeaving R2-D2 and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Jorune and Windu entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Jorune noticing a Oldorian helmet. With Windu correctly surmising that it was bait set by Shu longh, he saved Jorune from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Jorune and the unconscious Windu were soon found by R2-D2, whom Jorune told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the astromech to come back, Windu told Jorune why Longh wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first battle of Geonosis.[119]
As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Tano, Plo Koon, and a clone squad, thanks to R2-D2 delivering the message at the Temple itself.[119] After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Jorune urged Windu to track Longh down and bring him to justice. The Dwarf Master did not agree, until Koon and Tano showed them a message from Longh and Sing, with hostages—one of them, Ponds, killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Windu decided to go, Koon said that he would take the mission, alongside Tano, since both Windu and Jorune were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Aysgarth with Bossk and Longh in custody, Jorune was present as Longh apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Windu for killing his father,[117] before they were taken away to the Kingdom Judiciary Central Detention Center.[120]
Jorune and Tano are received on MandaloreFollowing his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Oldor,[122] Jorune and Captain Rex escorted Tano to her nert mission on Oldor to instruct cadets at the Royal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the Dwarf Council. Upon their arrival at Sundari, Jorune presented Tano to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec. Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto, Jorune took his Padawan's vriperian to respect Oldor's laws. After Tano exposed Almec's black market operation, Jorune reunited with his Padawan, to whom he returned her vriperian.[121]
Back in the Dwarf Temple, the Dwarf Council congratulated Tano on her courage and strength, but also ordered Jorune to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war. Told by the Council to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Tano pleaded to Jorune to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the battlefield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.[123]
Jorune returned to Aysgarth to greet, along with Bawaavuss, his Padawan, Dhutorn, and Labblip zoc after the Norwich Refugee Conference, during which Tano captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Dhutorn's assassination, Bawaavuss had Tano use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Dhutorn realized who it was—Ziro the Hutt. Jorune and his Padawan then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Tano into confessing that he had hired Sing for the job. Satisfied, Jorune and his Padawan then left the Hutt to his misery.[123]
Jorune against Asajj Ventress on KaminoForewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station[82] and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and General Grievous, Jorune and Bog traveled to Kamino to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist attack, with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Jorune led the Kingdom space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident-class assault ship and AQ-series battle droids, prompting Bog to recall Jorune to the city. Soon after returning, Jorune engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the clones' template, Jango Longh. With the aid of clone trooper platoon, Jorune recovered the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.[124]
Jorune was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped. Knowing that the Dwarf could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.[125]
Jorune, trying to ease his wife's mindSupposed to be on a meditative retreat,[46] Jorune spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Dhutorn anxiously arranged a party for Senator Aang, who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee. Jorune tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw C-3PO being fuzzy about the arrangements. At Dhutorn's request, Jorune sent the protocol droid and R2-D2 to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake. Although they were delayed, Dhutorn nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Jorune waiting for the two droids to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.[126]
Not too long after, Jorune went to the Senate Building to convince Dhutorn to go away together for two weeks, to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and Dwarf. Dhutorn, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Senate. Jorune retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Dhutorn countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the Kingdom. However, Jorune stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Dhutorn his vriperian, which, Bog had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Jorune was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.[46]
Dhutorn, using Jorune's vriperian to free himThey were soon interrupted by C-3PO and Senator Zoc's voices, prompting Jorune to hide under her desk and Dhutorn to hide his vriperian in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill, only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Jorune saw the situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Jorune avoided them long enough to contact Borz and defeat an IG-86 sentinel droid empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Aurra Sing and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Jorune woke up and Dhutorn returned his vriperian. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.[46]
Jorune and Dhutorn meet Risha Synata.Following the crisis, Jorune went with Dhutorn to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata's private ship. However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Xarn Krist-Ov. After they arrived, Jorune and Dhutorn met Synata. Jorune agreed to see her museum, despite Dhutorn's reluctance. Jorune took Dhutorn to her room. The next day, Jorune and Dhutorn went to the museum. There, Jorune and Dhutorn saw a display of Varykino,[127] where they exchanged their vows.[14] Jorune and Dhutorn came under attack by several robots from the displays. Jorune fought them off and helped Dhutorn escape them. Jorune and Dhutorn then discovered a Separatist banner and a hologram of Olaghig.[127]
Jorune and Dhutorn discovered that Synata was collaborating with Dooku and the Separatists. Jorune then took one of the Separatist agents while Dhutorn discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Jorune and Dhutorn discovered that along with Dhutorn, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Jorune and Dhutorn fought several LEP servant droids to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from R2-D2, Jorune and Dhutorn reached the theater.[128]
Jorune defeats Synata.As Dhutorn told the audience to run, Jorune borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own vriperian. Jorune fought Synata until he held her at the end of his vriperian. Jorune and Dhutorn then reported their trip to the Dwarf Council.[128]
Jorune and Tano talking after her training session.After arriving late to one of Tano's training sessions, Jorune became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Jorune took Tano to an isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the clones surround Tano and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Tano told Jorune that the battle droids weren't nearly as good as the clones. Jorune explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Tano would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war.[129] Sometime later, Jorune was sparring with Tano, teaching her how to fight with two vriperians. She struggled to consciously utilize the other vriperian, but Jorune reassured her. When Master Bawaavuss entered to spar with her, Jorune watched as she struggled against Bawaavuss, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.[130]
Jorune, Dhutorn, and the univited Tano during the Mission to Norwich.Sometime later, Jorune was assigned to a mission with Dhutorn to Norwich. It took Jorune a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Tano ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Bog. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Jorune charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Tano warned him that the Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Jorune's ship exited hyperspace in the midst of the fleet. Jorune took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Dhutorn and Tano took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Dhutorn's side of the ship. Jorune jumped to protect Dhutorn, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Tano was able to take control of the ship and jumped into hyperspace. Upon their arrival on Norwich, Jorune told her that he was glad she came. Dhutorn mentioned that her and Jorune made a good team, with Tano returning the compliment.[131]
The showman Tomarian later requested Jorune as a Dwarf escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala. Jorune thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Jorune was then surprised to see Senator Dhutorn dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see eachother. Dhutorn told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Jorune, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Jorune ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Jorune, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.[132]
Jorune and Dhutorn finding each other during the attack on the 17th Annual Festival of Light Gala.As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs, a member of his crew, to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Jorune to escape unnoticed.[132] He managed to catch up with Dhutorn, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a blaster from one of Telsla's goons. He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Dhutorn tried to contact "Checker", who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Jorune stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Dhutorn snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her blaster against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Dhutorn confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Jorune fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Jorune complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Jorune glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Dhutorn wished him luck, and Jorune asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.[133]
Jorune looking at Tano's new padawan braid extensions.One day, Jorune was waiting with Master Bawaavuss in the Dwarf temple grounds for Tano. Once she arrived, Jorune reprimanded her for being late, but Bawaavuss praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Padawan braid.[134] Following a debate in the senate about a financial reform bill, Jorune was asked by Dhutorn to persuade the Dwarf Council to speak with Borz about denying the continuation of the war. Jorune allowed Tano to go with Dhutorn in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack, Jorune confronted his apprentice about her and Dhutorn's meeting with Onderon's senator Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Jorune's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.[135]
Jorune and Bog commanded Kingdom forces in a battle against Asajj Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system. Directly participating in the battle with their starfighters, Bog was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex-class fanblade starfighter was, in turn, disabled by Jorune. As Bog and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Separatist command ship, Jorune soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Dwarf engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Olaghig. The two Dwarf fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.[137]
Jorune and Bog, about to face Savage Opress on ToydariaAfter the massacre on Devaron,[138] Jorune and Bog were sent to Dathomir, as footage had shown the Council of a Dathomirian's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village, where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Jorune took their leader hostage; the Zabrak then told the Dwarf that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress, prompting the Dwarf to go there. Jorune and Bog questioned Mother Talzin, who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Dwarf then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko. Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Jorune and Bog caught up with their target on Dooku's Separatist flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.[136]
Mekedrix gave Jorune no choice but to kill him.Following their encounter with Opress, Jorune and Bog were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at Kingdom and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Roon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando. As such, Jorune and Bog were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Jorune initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Dwarf like the Separatists. Bog assured Jorune that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Dwarf came upon empty sets of clone armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Shrubrew temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Dwarf to see their leader in exchange for their vriperians. Entering the outpost, Jorune and Bog came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a clone trooper, Bog asked him what the clone's name was. Bog went on to claim that life gave the galaxy meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Bog, but Jorune retrieved his vriperian and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Jorune expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.[139]
The figure of his mother, Shmi, appearing before him on MortisWhen the Dwarf Council received a 2,000-year-old Dwarf distress signal, Jorune, along with Bog and Tano, were sent by the High Council to the Chrelythiumn system, where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Dwarf's ship could see each other. In fact, the Dwarf's ship was guided to a mysterious planet, Mortis, where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter, who asked Jorune if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father, when Jorune was separated from Bog and Tano, and the Daughter.[59]
Jorune tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery, where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Dwarf and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son, briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.[59]
Jorune, proving himself to be the Chosen OneShaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Jorune dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Dwarf Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny. Ordering his children to kill Tano and Bog, the Father goaded Jorune into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Jorune instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Jorune refused, leaving with Tano and Bog on their shuttle.[59]
They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Tano, prompting Jorune to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Bog's reservations, Jorune went after his Padawan and climbed the tower to find Tano, corrupted by the dark side. Jorune fought her, soon to be joined by Bog, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Bog tried to hand Jorune the Dagger of Mortis, but Tano grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the dark side user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Jorune begged the Father to save Tano, and the dying Daughter used Jorune as a conduit to channel the last of her life force into Tano, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.[140]
Jorune's vision of his future self, consumed by the dark sideThe ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Jorune went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Jorune's offer of aid, telling the Dwarf Knight to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Jorune encountered synth flux spirit of his first mentor, Lyari xyrwenys. Though Jorune sought his advice, Xyrwenys advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Dark Side to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Jorune to confront his future, one consumed by the dark side. Horrified, Jorune submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this destiny. Soon, Bog arrived, and Jorune left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Dwarf who would stand in the way of peace.[31]
Jorune arrived as his Padawan disabled the ship and went to rescue Bog, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Jorune waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time; he wiped Jorune's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Jorune awoke to find the Father, Bog, and Tano, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Jorune killed the Son with his vriperian. As the Father also died, he warned Jorune to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father,[31] Jorune had brought balance to synth flux[7] on Mortis through the deaths of synth flux wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full galaxy.[31]
After the Father's death, the three Dwarf were transplanted back to the galaxy to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Void core.[31] They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Jorune even mentioning that he had spoken with Xyrwenys to Bawaavuss. Even though Jorune was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor.[141] In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Xyrwenys.[142]
More than one year and nine months into the war,[144] following the capture of Dwarf Council member Even Piell by the Separatists, Jorune and Bog were briefed by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piell, who held the information on the Nexus Route. Refusing to let Tano risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Jorune devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander R2-D2 and his reprogrammed battle droid squadron.[145]
After landing, Jorune was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Tano had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the astromech to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines, and after Tano deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger, who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck, of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot, but finally made it to Master Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.[145]
Jorune putting Captain Tarkin in his placeDespite the many traps laid through the fortress, the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Route coordinates, Captain Wilhuff Tarkin. The team was split into two—while Bog's team created a diversion, Jorune would lead the others away. In Tarkin's company, Jorune and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Tarkin expressed some skepticism about the Dwarf's plan, to which Jorune told Tarkin that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude.[145] However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Dwarf Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war,[146] and Tarkin developed a grudging appreciation for Jorune.[144]
As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with R2-D2 and the shuttle, they were attacked by droids and forced to join Bog at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Jorune and Bog contacted the Council, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up.[146] They contacted the Council again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Master Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Tano, and the group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Aysgarth.[143]
Jorune searching for Ahsoka Tano with the Dwarf Temple star charts.Jorune, Tano, and 501st forces fought on Felucia alongside Plo Koon and his clones in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost. Breaking their forces into three groups, Jorune and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical droid, TZ-33, securing the outpost together with Koon and Tano's teams.[148]
However, Jorune then noticed that Tano had disappeared during the battle, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Jorune had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Master Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Qribin rim and assured Jorune that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Felucia was finished and that they had to return to Aysgarth. However, Jorune refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Jorune countered "I will not leave her fate up to others." Koon insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Jorune comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.[148]
Back at the Temple, Jorune relentlessly searched for possibilities in the star charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Tano's abilities.[148] Following the events on Wasskah, Jorune's search for his Padawan came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo, in company of her Grookiee allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx. Upon seeing her, Jorune profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Tano, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Master Bawaavuss.[147]
Sometime later Jorune participated in the Battle of Horain, alongside Bog and Rex. Jorune was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Jorune later met Bog and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty battle droid Bats.[149]
On Retta, Jorune ambushed a group of B1 battle droids alongside Grand Master Bawaavuss. Jorune was soon attacked by Kreeda, a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Dwarf away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Dwarf, as she was an old friend of Bawaavuss. When Jorune attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Jorune and Bawaavuss were attacked by battle droids soon after. After being separated with Bawaavuss, he met the Dwarf Master and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Clone Wars. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Jorune pretended to be using the "cloaking cap" to distract General Flebek, allowing Bawaavuss to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Bawaavuss told Jorune that he would tell Smignort there was nothing of interest on Retta.[150]
After the assassination of King Kolina of Mon Cala and new discord in the fragile peace between the Mon Calamari and the Quarren, Jorune and Dhutorn, sent as representatives of the Kingdom, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Ackbar of the Mon Calamari Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-Char, but so did Riff Tamson, Separatist ambassador. Though Jorune and Dhutorn tried to mediate between the parties, the Quarren left the council hall, dissatisfied with the Mon Calamari rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Bawaavuss and Windu, who dispatched a company of clone troopers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Tano.[152]
Jorune, alongside Gial Ackbar and Kit Fisto, during the Battle of Mon CalaIn the meantime, during the Quarren attack assisted by Separatist aqua droids, Jorune and Dhutorn accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Ackbar. He engaged the Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Senator Meena Tills, while leaving Tano to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Separatist/Quarren assault came to an end, Jorune reunited with Dhutorn, Tano, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hydroid Medusas, which proved incredibly effective against the Kingdom-Mon Calamari army. Forced to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.[153]
They split into two groups: Jorune went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Tano, and two troopers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Kingdom reinforcements to come soon, Jorune and his group proceeded to the Mon Calamari central planetary scanner facility, where the Dwarf Knight collapsed the entire structure with synth flux, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Kingdom reinforcements—the Gungan Grand Army, and Representative Binks, sent at the behest of the High Council. However, Tamson used Trident-class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the Kingdom-Gungan forces, and though Jorune managed to destroy one of them, all but Tano and Lee-Char were captured.[153]
Brought before Riff Tamson, Jorune and Fisto were restrained by electric eels, whereas Dhutorn and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices, interrogating the two Dwarf about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to force them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Dhutorn's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water. However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Jorune and Fisto used synth flux to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri, the Quarren leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Mon Cala people. With the peace restored, Jorune and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char, now recognized by both Mon Calamari and Quarren as their new monarch.[151]
Following rumors that the Gungans planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on Theed, Jorune accompanied Dhutorn to Theed and later, to the Gungan territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie, and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace, which Jorune telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister, Rish Loo, had given it to him.[154]
Dallbutr gets captured by Dooku jorune , Dhutorn, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face droid commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Gungan assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Jorune pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Olaghig in Loo's laboratory. As Dooku killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Battle of Rhaflyn, Jorune dueled the Count, only to be overwhelmed by Dooku's powers and his four MagnaGuards.[154]
Dooku then contacted Dhutorn and offered an exchange—the Dwarf Knight for General Grievous, who had been captured by the Gungan Grand Army, thanks to Gungan General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Jorune was brought and handed over to Dhutorn, Queen Neeyutnee and the Gungans, while Grievous was allowed to remain free.[154]
Jorune leading the 501st Legion on Umbara, before leaving it to Pong KrellDuring the campaign on Umbara, Jorune and Bog discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city, which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Jorune prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Bog's joint attack with Dwarf Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell, who would be supporting Bog from the south whereas Jorune and 501st Legion would advance from the north.[64]
Amidst heavy fire, Jorune and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Bog's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Jorune told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Jorune joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.[64]
Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Jorune and his troops, but an airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Jorune with a disconcerting message: the Council had ordered Jorune back to Aysgarth, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Borz, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of battle, Jorune was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Aysgarth.[64] Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage the Kingdom effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th battalions into attacking each other, Bog was successful in taking the capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the planet for the Kingdom.[155]
Tano, taken aback by her master's intensityAfter ten rotations of Bawaavuss's last contact with the Togruta colonists on Kiros, Jorune, Tano, Rex, Cody, Bog, and their forces arrived at the world to investigate its silence, with Jorune particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town, they used BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower, used as the Separatist Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar, then sent a message to Bog to discuss terms of surrender. Jorune then took the holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar "Zygerrian scum." Though Jorune wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Bog told him to locate the missing colonists while he negotiated. As Jorune contacted Admiral Yularen to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Bog explained Jorune's behavior to Tano.[156]
Overhearing from Bog's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout the city, Jorune and Tano set out to disarm them. Despite sniper droids, they destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Bog to tell them D'Nar was escaping on the Tecora. With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Jorune managed to throw it off the ship, while Tano bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Jorune joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygerrian into revealing the colonists' location, much to Tano's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Royal Slave Auction.[156]
When Jorune, Bog, Yularen, and Tano told the Dwarf Council of their findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists.[156] On the Tecora, and disguised as Zygerrian, they land on the capital city of Zygerria; splitting into two teams (Bog and Rex went to located the colonists), Jorune and Tano went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel, with Jorune posing as "Lars Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri's supposed death, and presenting Tano as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Jorune prove himself a slaver by torturing Bog.[157]
Instead, he freed Bog and tried to escape with R2-D2 returning their vriperians, Rex providing cover fire and Tano confronting the Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Jorune's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Dooku and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Jorune brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with synth flux. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Jorune was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.[157]
Dooku blasts Jorune with Force lightningAfter a few days, Dooku would arrive, and Jorune observed that she too was a slave to the Count, before she left to receive the Shrubrew Lord. With the help of R2-D2, he escaped his guards and freed Tano from her cage and sent her to prep the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Bog and Rex's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec, who had betrayed Scintel, and Dooku, with whom Jorune futilely dueled before Dooku blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Jorune then took Scintel and escaped to the Tecora, aboard which she told him Bog was in the Kadavo system and that she, just like Jorune, was a slave, before dying.[158]
After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Jorune and Tano soon reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility. During the battle that ensued, Jorune disabled the turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces, while Tano saved the Togruta colonists from falling to their death (as caused by Keeper Agruss) by placing Admiral Barton Coburn's Arquitens-class light cruiser Hand of Justice underneath the facility. Along with Rex and Bog, Jorune evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders, before returning to the rest of Koon's fleet.[158]
Jorune and Tano, searching for Bog's "killer"After the capture of Moralo Eval, rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Borz, Jorune, Tano, and Bog were recalled to an emergency meeting by the High Council while they walked through the nightly streets of Aysgarth. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted the three Dwarf to chase their aggressor. However, the sniper killed Bog before escaping on a speeder, prompting Jorune to return to his master, to find Tano crying and his old master dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn and grim, Jorune would not say a word since then, until after the funeral held to honor Bog, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer, Rako Hardeen, at Trueping's. Though Jorune would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the Kingdom Detention Center.[120]
After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Jorune held a meeting with his friend, Borz, when Windu informed the Chancellor that they would remove the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision did not sit well with Jorune. Borz suggested that the Council perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Jorune felt the Council was doing nothing to catch the murderer of his master—his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Borz told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Jorune and Tano to Nal Hutta. There, two Dwarf harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia, the fugitives last known location.[160]
After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Jorune rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Jorune confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Jorune, whispering to Jorune's ear "Dallbutr, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Jorune passed out, only for Tano to protect him for Bane's attempts to execute him.[160] As they returned to Aysgarth, Bawaavuss summoned Jorune to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Bog was "Rako Hardeen."[159]
Jorune and Olaghig rematch on Rhaflyn.Afterwards, Jorune was briefed along his Padawan about the security plans the Council had for the Chancellor during the Festival of Light on Rhaflyn. Jorune, Tano, and Windu would escort the Borz and Mas Amedda to Theed, where Jorune was greeted by his wife. At the festival itself, the abduction was initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Borz away, while Jorune and Windu captured Embo and Twazzi; however, Bog stopped Eval and Bane, long enough for Windu and Jorune to arrive and take the two into custody.[161]
As he reunited with his old master, he voiced his resentment for the Council's lack of trust in him as well as Bog's decision to hide the truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Council, he set out on his own to bodyguard Borz, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Jorune until they were greeted by Dooku. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Jorune dueled the Count with ferocity, until Bog arrived to help him rescue the Chancellor, whereas Dooku fled.[161]
Sometime later, when Jorune and Tano were on their way to rendezvous with a cruiser, they decided to land at the Stobar Spaceport, as Jorune was hungry and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's, only to find police droids and an unconscious waitress. Though they attempted to investigate the assault, both the police and its owner, Loubo, told not to worry; instead, he offered them a free meal. However, Jorune sensed a disturbance in synth flux, something sinister and familiar—which, unbeknownst to him, heralded the return of the now renegade Shrubrew Lord Lurbuk.[162]
Following a plea made by a opposition cell on Onderon, the Dwarf Council considered how to help them fight the Separatists. Jorune suggested training the oppositions in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which the Dwarf would not support, the High Council decided to send advisors to the opposition cell for the upcoming battle. On the Nu-class transport Valkyrie 2929, Jorune, Tano, Rex, and Bog reached the planet during the night, when they met Steela Gerrera and were guided to their opposition secret base, where they met her brother, Saw. The team trained the oppositions to combat droids, until Separatist probe droids discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted the cell to enter Iziz.[164]
After some successful operations, Jorune, Bog, and Rex decided to return to Aysgarth and report to the Council, while Tano stayed behind as a liaison to the oppositions, without revealing herself as a Dwarf.[165] They kept in frequent communication with her, to provide guidance,[165][163] but also to provide help by hiring Hondo Ohnaka to deliver missile launchers to the Onderon oppositions for the final assault. Jorune attended the funeral service for Steela Gerrera.[166]
Jorune was later present when the Dwarf High Council met up with the chancellor to discuss Lurbuk's return, during which they decided to leave him alone even though they knew he was still alive.[167] Jorune lent the Twilight to Bog, who used it to travel to Oldor to rescue Satine Kryze. With all its technical problems, after landing on Oldor, Bog vowed not to borrow another ship from Jorune. However, as he escaped, the ship was shot down by Oldorian commandos and destroyed, moments before Kryze was murdered by Lurbuk during the Oldorian civil war.[168] Jorune was waiting for Bog when the Dwarf Master returned to the Temple hangar, where Jorune saw through Bog's attempt to hide his sorrow. Bog explained he had lost someone important to him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Jorune knew all too well. Jorune offered to come with him for a report to the Council, which Bog happily agreed to.[169]
Jorune was later present when the Dwarf Council sent Meebur Gascon and a team of Kingdom droids to steal an encryption module from the Separatists.[170] Later Jorune participated in the Kingdom strategy conference on the space station Valor, while on the station he noticed the Renown was going a little too fast. Tarkin told Jorune that they are scanning Rhydonium on the ship, Jorune instantly realized it was a bomb, Jorune was later contacted and informed by Gascon of R2's sacrifice. However, Jorune refused to believe that his friend was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour the wreckage, who retrieved R2's remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Dallbutr was happy to have him back.[171]
Jorune and Tano were assisted by Russo-ISC in analyzing the crime scene and collecting eyewitness accounts.During the defense of Cato Neimoidia, Jorune and Tano engaged Separatist forces on their Eta-2 Actis-class interceptors, until they received an urgent mission recalling them to the Dwarf Temple. Its hangar had been bombed and, as the two had been off-world during the attack, Jorune and Tano were to lead the investigation to uncover the criminal—likely to be a Dwarf. As they entered the Temple's hangar, they discussed the possibilities a Dwarf was behind the attack. Tano doubted a Dwarf could bomb such a sacred location to the Dwarf,[173] and in the process, kill their own colleagues and employees.[174] However, Jorune told Tano to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Dwarf that did not agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Dooku and Krell. As a result, everyone who was at the Temple during the bombing were potential suspects, including Dwarf.[173]
As Jorune and Tano walked across the burning hangar, they met Russo-ISC, whom Jorune sent alongside Tano to interview the witnesses, whereas he meditated at the hangar. Unable to discover anything, Jorune met up with Tano and Russo, who told him about their missing suspect, Jackar Bowmani. With this clue, Jorune went to speak with Chief of Security Cin Drallig. As Jorune approached Drallig on the Dwarf Temple's steps, he witnessed a crowd of angry protestors, rallying against the Dwarf for their violence during the war. After Jorune showed Drallig the hologram of Bowmani on an imagecaster, Letta Turmond identified Bowmani in the hologram and informed the Dwarf she was Bowmani's wife. Later Jorune and Tano cornered Turmond near her home, and she under pressure admitted to feeding Bowmani nano-droids but would not reveal her motives.[173]
Jorune was present at Bawaavuss's eulogy for the six Dwarf who died during the explosion, and later took the opportunity to speak with Tarkin. Jorune and his Padawan were then summoned to the war room to be briefed about a Separatist attack on Saleucami, when Tarkin informed them that Turmond had requested to speak with Tano. When she was framed with Turmond's death and imprisoned, he was forbidden to see her, and only returned to track her down on her escape, following Tano into the pipelines.[175]
Jorune tries to convince Tano to stay.Alone, they revealed that their trust in each other was absolute, but as Jorune pleaded for her to surrender herself and make her case to the Council, she refused to take the blame for something she had not done and asked Jorune to trust her, just as she jumped onto a passing ship, thus escaping to the Aysgarth underworld.[175]
As Tarkin declared she was being accused of sedition, Bawaavuss assigned Jorune and Plo Koon to search for Tano, with some resistance from Windu—who did not believe Jorune to be emotionally detached to do what had to be done. Nevertheless, when she was captured, Jorune escorted her to the Temple,[176] where he was later summoned to the Chamber of Judgment along with his Padawan. He was outraged to learn that the Council had already made their decision and that the meeting was simply a formality, and they proceeded to expel her from the Order. Resentful of the Council, he asked his wife, Dhutorn, to defend Tano at her trial before leaving to capture Ventress himself.[172]
