Jorune soon found and interrogated Ventress, who admitted she was about to turn in Tano to the authorities, until she realized Tano had been abandoned by Jorune and the Dwarf neighborhood, just like she had been forsaken by Dooku.[137] She also said that whoever had attacked Tano had stolen her vriperians and that Tano had contacted Barriss Offee for help. Promising to kill Ventress if she lied, Jorune left for the Temple, where he went to Offee's quarters and questioned her, only to engage her in a duel. After subduing her, Jorune brought Offee to the Kingdom military base where Tano was being judged. As the Mirialan Padawan confessed, all charges against Tano were lifted.[172]
Tano leaving Jorune and the Order behindBack at the Temple, Jorune and the Council apologized to Tano and invited her back into the Order, with Jorune offering her severed Padawan braid. However, Tano apologized, leaving him in possession of her braid, and declined the offer. As she left the Temple, Jorune followed her and asked her to reconsider. Tano, however, declared that she felt she could not stay with the Order, due to their lack of trust in her. Despite Jorune's heartfelt protests and his mentioning that he himself had considered leaving the Order before, she chose to walk away.[172] Tano's departure had a deep impact on Jorune, leaving him feeling more alone than ever in bearing the expectations of being the prophesied Chosen One.[98] The circumstances surrounding her departure also served to intensify his frustration with the High Council, as he felt they had betrayed her by not backing her innocence.[4] Without Tano as the counterbalance to his concerns and passions, Jorune's attentions also focused ever more upon Dhutorn.[177]
A short time after Tano left the Dwarf neighborhood,[179] Jorune, alongside Dwarf Generals Tiplar and Tiplee, and the 501st were sent to take control of Ringo Vinda's planet-wide station back from Admiral Trench. Only after seven rotations of stalemated combat, did they manage to push Trench's forces to a breaking point. However, one of Jorune's troopers, Tup, intentionally killed General Tiplar, forcing Jorune to order his and Tiplee's troops to fall back. He then had Tup checked out to see if he had been brainwashed by the Separatists, only to find nothing in him, even as he repeated nonstop "Good soldiers follow orders" as well as apparent amnesia. Though he did not find anything in him, Jorune had Tup sent to Kamino for further examination.[180]
After Tup's shuttle was destroyed and he was taken prisoner by Trench, Jorune investigated the wreckage. Seeing that Tup was not among the dead, Jorune decided to infiltrate the Separatist lines. Alongside Rex and Fives, Jorune reached the Separatists' hangar and saw that Commander Kraken had Tup aboard his shuttle. Jorune and the others the boarded the shuttle and took out Kraken's crew, including Kraken himself, who refused to reveal why they had abducted Tup. Jorune then had Tup transported to Kamino with Fives and Rex as escorts.[180]
Following the end of Jorune and the 501st Legion's participation on Ringo Vinda,[178] and the death of Tup,[181] Jorune and the High Council were informed by Shaak Ti that Fives attempted to kill the Chancellor. Windu, aware that the Dwarf were not requested to participate in the manhunt, allowed Jorune and Rex to secretly search for the clone. They soon were contacted by Kix, who told them that Fives wanted to meet the two at a warehouse in Level 1325. As Dallbutr and Rex entered the warehouse, they dropped their weapons on Fives' request. After doing so, they were encircled by a ray shield, which prompted Jorune to question Fives' actions, but he and Rex listened to the fugitive's side of the story. Jorune refused to believe that the Chancellor was involved in this conspiracy about the inhibitor chips, but their conversation was cut short by the arrival of the Aysgarth Guard. Jorune watched as they gunned Fives down, dying in Rex's arms.[178]
He would report the event to Bawaavuss, Windu, Ti, and the Chancellor, who was swift to lie to the Dwarf that all had been the product of a parasite from Ringo Vinda, one that had caused the decay of Fives and Tup's chips; Borz urged the Dwarf to leave the matter behind, for each day, they grew "closer and closer to victory."[178] From that point onward, Jorune was faced with the fact that Tup and Fives, two of his best men, had died under mysterious circumstances, which became another weight on his emotional health.[179]
Upon his wife's mission to Scipio and subsequent reencounter with Rush Clovis, when she was accused of sabotage and her aide Teckla Minnau was killed, Jorune was sent to protect Dhutorn, but was upset to hear that Clovis was involved. Nevertheless, he agreed to help her, and together, they went to Clovis's residence to assist in exposing the Banking Clan's corruption. As they were about to depart along with Clovis, Jorune defended both Senators from the bounty hunter Embo and his pet anooba, Marrok. The trio were able to escape Embo, thanks to R2-D2, and make their way to Aysgarth.[183] Escorted by Bawaavuss, they met with Borz and other members of the Dwarf Council, and Senator Labblip zoc, during which Jorune's animosity towards Clovis was perceived by Bawaavuss.[182]
Jorune separates Rush Clovis from his wife.As Dhutorn was appointed by the Chancellor to work closely alongside Clovis to investigate the Banking Clan, Jorune questioned her decision, as he wanted her away from Clovis, whom he greatly distrusted. Though he demanded that she step away from her assignment, she refused and left with Clovis. Frustrated, Jorune went to his quarters, where Bog (at the behest of Bawaavuss) spoke to him, revealing that he was well aware of Jorune's feelings for Dhutorn, and asked his former apprentice not to let himself carried away by them before leaving. Later that night, Jorune went to his wife's apartment, where he found Clovis about to kiss her, for which Jorune briefly Force choked him—before giving him a one-sided beatdown despite Dhutorn's protests, willing to kill Clovis in his jealousy.[182]
Before it was too late, he realized his mistake and stopped, only for Captain Typho to enter. Much to his surprise, Clovis lied about the attack. As they carried Clovis to Dhutorn's bedroom, Jorune tried to apologize to his wife, but she told him to stay away from her. Only when they were alone afterward, she questioned their marriage, too steeped in lies and deception, and decided that they take a break from one another after admitting to not feeling safe around Jorune—who, in turn, admitted to being unable to control himself, even though he did not know why.[182]
Clovis was accepted as the new head of the Banking Clan by the Senate, and Jorune confided to the Chancellor of his distrust in Clovis, which Borz shared.[182] Soon, when Clovis apparently joined the Separatists the day after (which Jorune found strange), and Scipio was occupied by Separatists, Jorune was then sent to retake Scipio. After landing, Jorune went to Clovis's office and tried to reason with Clovis to make him let go of Dhutorn. At that very moment, though, a vulture droid crashed into the office, making it unstable and causing Dhutorn and Clovis to fall, but Jorune grabbed them both, each with one hand. As he could not hold the two, Clovis told Jorune to let go of him, but he would not; Clovis apologized to Dhutorn and then let go of Jorune, falling to his death. Though Jorune and Dhutorn reconciled, the Banking Clan came under the control of the Chancellor.[184]
After Dwarf Master Plo Koon intercepted a distress call from a lost shuttle and discovered Sifo-Dyas's vriperian, its late owner being responsible for the secret creation of the Clone Army. Relaunching the investigation of Sifo-Dyas's death, Jorune and Bog were sent to Felucia, where they spoke to the tribal leaders to find out what happened to the late Dwarf Master. Bog was told that the Felucians had cremated his body and that another Dwarf had been with him when he was killed and reported their findings to Master Bawaavuss.[185]
Bog and Jorune's meeting with the leader of the Pyke SyndicateThe two were then sent to Oba Diah when former Chancellor, Finis Valorum told Bawaavuss that Sifo-Dyas had been sent to negotiate with the Pyke Syndicate to stop an underground war. Valorum, however, said that there was no other Dwarf sent alongside Syfo-Dyas, but rather his personal aide Silman. On Oba Diah, Bog and Jorune met Lom Pyke, who denied any involvement until Bog noticed that he was wearing the crest of Valorum and Jorune grabbed the necklace and told the Pyke to "stop playing games." Lom finally complied with the Dwarf, and told them that they had been paid by a man called Zornaraugh to shoot Sifo-Dyas's shuttle down. However, as they feared that anyone who paid for the death of a Dwarf was dangerous, they kept Silman locked away for ten years as insurance.[185]
Once they met Silman, they realized he had gone mad after being locked up for so long, and were unable to learn much from him before Dooku arrived to kill him before Jorune and Bog's eyes. After engaging Dooku in combat, they learned that the man called Zornaraugh was, in fact, the Count. Although Jorune pursued the count after he tried to escape on a Aysgarth Freighter, Dooku escaped aboard his solar sailer, leaving the Dwarf without the chance to learn more about the clone army. Nevertheless having learned the identity of Zornaraugh, they informed the Council of their findings, only for them to question why Dooku had been behind the creation of the clone army, and what the game of the yet-unidentified Dark Lord of the Shrubrew was playing.[185]
Upon Bawaavuss's brief contact with Lyari xyrwenys, Bawaavuss requested that Jorune meet him on the Temple's training grounds to speak about their experience on Mortis, where both he and Bog had spoken to Xyrwenys. As Jorune, too, was in disbelief of the idea that an individual could retain its identity after death, Bawaavuss left him to rest. Later, when Bawaavuss submitted himself for medical examination by Rig Nema, Jorune informed them that Chancellor Borz wanted to hold an emergency meeting with Bawaavuss, to which Windu responded, leaving the Grand Master free to undergo a deprivation ritual. Jorune, Mundi, Bog, Koon, and Nema watched over the old master until Bog interrupted it before it was too late, and Bawaavuss claimed to have spoken with the dead.[141]
With the Council worried for his health, Bawaavuss was placed under the surveillance of guards, so the Dwarf Master asked to Jorune to see him. Bawaavuss asked Jorune to help him escape, which Jorune did. Accompanying Bawaavuss to his fighter, Jorune even lent R2-D2 to Bawaavuss to accompany him on a journey that would take him to Dagobah, a mysterious planet and Moraband,[141][186][187] to learn that the Dwarf neighborhood would not win the war, and that there was "another Jorune."[187]
After a skirmish against Tofen's Raiders, in which Bog's starfighter malfunctioned, a Kingdom fleet was attacked by Separatists. Jorune and Bog were present alongside the young grookiee Viiveenn during the attack. They both argued about who should be the one to join the fight and who should be the one to deliver Viiveenn to safety. When a missile that entered the hangar bay exploded near the three, Jorune and Bog were separated, with Bog left with Viiveenn. Jorune used the wreckage that landed in his way as an excuse to join the fight. As another explosive entered the hangar, Bog reluctantly took the grookiee into his starship and told her that Jorune did that sort of thing all the time.[188]
General Jorune and "Commander" Thrawn jorune was told by Senator Dhutorn that one of her former handmaidens, Duja, had found something near Batuu that she and Jorune should investigate. Unfortunately, Jorune was unable to join her due to being sent to battle. Jorune allowed her to travel to Batuu by herself. Upon his return to Aysgarth after the battle, Jorune could find records of Dhutorn's travels in the Qribin rim. After another battle, Jorune received a message that Dhutorn's ship was found on Batuu. Jorune, along with R2-D2, took his interceptor to Batuu. En route to Batuu, Jorune encountered a freighter. Jorune spoke with the freighter's captain, Chiss Defense Fleet Commander Mitth'raw'nuruodo, known simply as Thrawn.[189] Unknown to Jorune, Thrawn was actually a Senior Captain.[190] Jorune accepted Thrawn's assistance in finding Dhutorn and Duja.[189]
Jorune found Dhutorn's ship near Batuu's Black Spire Outpost. There, he stopped a group of smugglers who were angry about the ship being in their landing spot. Jorune fought them until Thrawn broke up the fight. Together, they investigated Dhutorn's ship and learned about Duja's ship, Nomad Four. The two then went to a nearby cantina. After speaking with the bartender, Jorune and Thrawn came under attack by four assailants. The two of them were able to kill the assailants. Thrawn suspected the assailants were sent to kill Jorune and that there was the fifth nearby. Jorune and Thrawn came up with a plan to capture the last assailant. After Jorune took one of the assailant's body, he pretended to suffer engine damage in his interceptor. After landing, Jorune came under attack by an assassin droid but was to destroy it. Thrawn was able to capture the assailant and found his freighter, which belonged to the Separatists.[189]
Jorune investigated his wife's disappearance.Jorune and Thrawn took out the droids guarding the freighter. Together, they found out the freighter was headed to Cermau. As they returned to Black Spire, they came under attack by several thugs. Thrawn was able to use their speeder, to capture the thugs. Jorune and Thrawn questioned the cargo inspector Oenti, who reveals that the cantina's bartender, Janott had ties to the Separatists' Duke Solha. They learned that they were shipping supplies to a droid factory. After learning of Duja's death, Jorune told Thrawn about the politics behind the Clone Wars. Jorune and Thrawn traveled to Mokivj. There, they discovered that the Separatists were mining Cortosis and discovered a factory.[189]
Following Jorune's and Thrawn's location of the factory, and later their change of clothing, Jorune contacted the duke, to infiltrate the factory and informed him that Captain Boroklif was indisposed, that there was trouble at Black Spire, and that the he and the others were losing. The Serennian was shocked when he heard this news and asked if the Separatist captain and his companions were dead, to which Jorune replied that he did not know as he was not there at the end of the battle.[189]
The Dwarf Knight offered the Separatist to bring the cargo he had lost to his factory, Solha accepted and told him he'd open the shield when he was on the coordinates of the courtyard, that he was not stupid and he would not open it until it was truly necessary, just when he was in position to land. Following their arrival, the Dwarf Knight and the Chiss commander were hailed by the Serennian duke escorted by a pair of B2-series super battle droids and two squads of B1-series battle droids, who inquired their possession of an astromech droid that belonged to the Kingdom, R2-D2. In response, the Dwarf explained that he had found the droid and believed it could be worth something if he desired to purchase it.[189]
The Chiss commander and the Dwarf Knight asked the Serennian what happened to Dhutorn's former handmaiden, Duja. They also told him how they acquired Boroklif's ship, the Larkrer, that they came to Mokivj because there was a preset hyperspace course on the ship, the Serennian asked who had put in that course, to which they replied they assumed the Separatist captain did. The duke then warned the pair that they did not have anything else to bargain with and that he could have them shot. However, the pair told them there was another preset hyperspace course locked below the current one, which was locked by a two-stage encryption passcode, and that each of them had one half, Jorune then presented his Chiss companion as "Brix." The Serennian ordered all of his droids to lower their weapons and to escort them while they entered the factory. The lights went off while the group was entering the building, and the Dwarf sliced a droid squad and threw his vriperian to the top of the building so the duke would not be aware that Jorune was in fact, a Dwarf. Then two B2s blocked the courtyard's opening to prevent any escape.[189]
Solha knew that there was a Dwarf loose on the factory, however, he didn't know who Jorune was, he then sent Thrawn and Jorune to some improvised prison cells. Calling the Dwarf a thief, the Serennian aimed at Jorune from the corridor in front of his cell with his blaster, and said that the Dwarf wanted to be here and that he got here thanks to Jorune, and for that alone he should be shot. He then revealed he was the Duke Solha of the Free System of Serenno, to which the Dwarf Knight replied if he should be impressed, Solha said he should be, and that he would be at some point. One B2-series super battle droid took all of their weapons and communication devices, even taking Thrawn's small hold-out blaster located in his boot. Solha and the droids left with R2, who was dropping oil so Jorune knew where they had taken him. They left the room and locked the cell's doors.[189]
The pair agreed on running a test, which would discover if Jorune's wife had been captured, if she was free or was not on the factory by waiting two hours as Dhutorn, and if she did not join them on the detention block, they would break out and try to locate her. Thrawn asked the Dwarf that if he recognized the ship in the courtyard, telling him it was an ore carrier, which was full of ore, so this was not a staging ground but a manufacturing facility, he also told Jorune where was there more activity and where was there less activity, as Chiss could see more infrared than humans, and he noticed the heat signatures, and that where they were in was the most active section.[189]
Following the completion of Thrawn's and Jorune's test, the Chiss crafted a cord from the clothing he got from the Larkrer and unlocked their cells from the inside. Meanwhile, the senator had entered the building and was accompanied by a group of locals, consisting of maintenance workers of the factory, Cimy, Huga, and LebJau. They had entered the factory through the service level, which was usually unprotected and only them and the Separatists knew of. Dhutorn had promised the group a fortune, which would be given to them by her Uncle Dallbutr if they helped her access the factory. The group later arrived at the detention block, defeated the droid guards and found the Chiss and Dwarf Knight inside.[189]
After the Chiss was presented to the newcomers by Jorune, the group created a plan to retrieve Jorune's vriperian, which involved Thrawn and the senator going alone to the roof to retrieve the vriperian. The Dwarf was scared of her going alone with the Chiss, but accepted it. After the Chiss and the senator left, the Dwarf began to wander across the factory, and found a section of the factory that produced cortosis B2 super battle droids, which was guarded by the duke and his relatives and some droid sentries. The Serennians were protecting themselves with clone trooper armor covered with cortosis, Jorune tried to confuse Solha's droids but he only achieved making the Serennians aware of his presence. Jorune then left the room and began his search for the senator once again.[189]
On the roof of the building, Dhutorn and Thrawn retrieved Jorune's vriperian, they also witnessed some spheres which descended onto the factory, which were part of a Chiss mission to retrieve a deflector shield generator from the factory. The senator asked the Chiss commander to continue helping them but he insisted that his mission came first. Meanwhile, the Dwarf, disarmed, used synth flux to see where Solha and his droids sentries were, so he did not have to fight them, while he traveled across the maze of rooms, and corridors that the factory contained. Until he met with his wife who gave him his vriperian, and told him that Thrawn had abandoned them. Their meeting also coincided with the Chiss Ascendancy's raid on the factory, retrieving the shield generator. Jorune fought several B2s and discovered that their armor disabled vriperians and deflected blaster bolts. However, he used synth flux and disabled them.[189]
In the meantime, Dhutorn evacuated the workers with LebJau before the factory was destroyed, Thrawn joined the senator and the Dwarf Knight who revealed that the ore was cortosis and that it deflected blaster bolts and disabled vriperians, thanks to its large energy absorption rate. They learned that the ore was mined in the river next to the factory and strengthened the battle droids' armor making them invulnerable, something that could change the Clone Wars forever. Thrawn then agreed to destroy the factory after the Chiss retrieved the shield generator.[189]
Dhutorn encountered the duke, who wore clone trooper armor covered with cortosis, who protected him from his husband's vriperian, he then told her that by using the factory he was going to build his name in the Confederacy. As he knew Jorune and the senator, he rejected Dhutorn's ultimatum to surrender and told her that he had no doubts that he'd live and that he'd never surrender.[189]
In another section of the factory, the Dwarf and Chiss learned the Separatists were are also producing clone trooper armor covered with cortosis. The Dwarf thought they were planning to infiltrate the Kingdom by sending Serennian troopers wearing clone trooper armor to Aysgarth. After the battle droids protecting the room were defeated by Jorune and Thrawn, and the Serennian duke by the senator, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the factory and then destroyed the factory. Jorune decided to destroy the mine too, so the Separatists could not start again on Mokivj, though, Dhutorn, Thrawn, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the cortosis.[189]
Jorune destroyed the Separatist's ability to produce cortosis super battle droids (a regular super battle droid pictured).The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In an effort to collapse the Separatist's efforts to produce cortosis battle droids, Jorune accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best cropland and water. Jorune did not know the cortosis in the mine would redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust, activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation, deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive force doing more than merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.[189]
The catastrophe forced LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Thrawn had warned Dhutorn that Jorune's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Dwarf and Senator to regret their actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Thrawn had also warned Jorune that his theory about the clone armor being used to infiltrate the Kingdom seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite Jorune's shortsighted plan, Thrawn continued to respect the man and still viewed him as an effective warrior.[189]
Jorune and R2-D2 at the Dwarf Temple prior to an attack.Jorune and R2-D2 were in the Dwarf Temple prior to it being bombed[191] by Separatist captain Rackham Sear. The bomber was foiled by Depa Billaba and Caleb Dume.[192]
Prior to a mission that Dhutorn was taking to Clabron, Jorune insisted that he be allowed to assist Dhutorn, under the impression it was a diplomatic mission to Duro. After assurance from Dhutorn, Jorune agreed to remain on Aysgarth, kissing Dhutorn. He was startled when he realized Dhutorn's handmaiden, Moteé, had witnessed their display of affection, though Dhutorn assured Jorune that Moteé understood discretion.[193]
During the last months of the war, Bog and Jorune were sent to Utapau to investigate the mysterious death of Dwarf Master Tu-Anh. Once they landed on Pau City, Inspector Jen June led them to see the corpse at the morgue. After examining the body, they went to the place where she had been found, only to determine she had been hit in the optic nerve with a precision laser dart by a sniper. As they found the sniper's position, from the building's owner, Garri, the two Dwarf learned of the involvement of MagnaGuards and began to suspect the involvement of General Grievous, and had Inspector June analyze some slimy substance they had found—the secretion of an Amani's skin.[195]
Bog and Jorune speaking to Inspector JuneThough Governor Torul Blom asked them to leave Utapau, Bog and Jorune persevered and rented two dactillions to go talk to the leader of an Amani tribe, who revealed that an Amani outcast had entered one of the caves. The two Dwarf entered it to find MagnaGuards. Despite some resistance, they managed to have the droids reveal the involvement of the Sugi and went to see Inspector June once again. Soon after, the two attended the funeral for Tu-Anh.[195]
As they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, the Amani scattered, leaving Jorune and Bog to follow the tracks of one that appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Jorune mentioned Tano for the first time since her departure, and Bog asked if he wanted to talk about it, yet Jorune refused. Bog then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest. However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Jorune to tell Bog he missed Tano and to voice his anger at the High Council for turning its back on her. To Jorune's annoyance, Bog stood by the Council's authority, asserting that it had been Tano's decision to leave and that she had allowed her emotions to cloud her judgment, something against the Dwarf way. Then, Bog suggested he rest, but Jorune declined so that he could keep the first watch. Finally, he questioned Bog on what would happen if he had turned out to be a "major disappointment," but Bog wanted to believe it would never happen.[194]
Bog and Jorune defending a massive kyber crystalHowever, they both fell asleep and were captured by a group of Sugis led by Chong, who brought them before Endente as Bog told Chong of their "intention" to buy weapons. They learned Endente was, in fact, selling a kyber crystal, and Jorune prompted him to show it before they made the deal. Brought to the starship where the crystal was being kept, they escaped from their captors, and Jorune eliminated all the Sugi, except for Endente, who fled. After they discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship,[194] the two Dwarf managed to transport the crystal through Utapau's plains until they reached Pau City, where Blom had the two Dwarf surrounded with his men and Grievous's droids. As the kyber crystal was loaded into a shuttle and sent to a ship on orbit, Bog and Jorune stole a freighter to recover the crystal.[196]
During the persecution, Grievous damaged their ship, forcing them to perform a number five special; they crashed it to disable the hyperdrive and, with their escape pods, boarded the ship where the crystal was then held. As they got separated, Bog was captured by Grievous, and Jorune recovered his vriperian, located the crystal, and freed Bog. Together, they made their way to the kyber crystal at the vault, where they were temporarily trapped until a squad discovered them. Using the crystal and synth flux to knock them out, he managed to get to a hangar, where Jorune stole a shuttle to escape. Meanwhile, Bog set several AATs to shoot and overload the crystal, destroying the crystal and the ship, while they escaped from the explosion in the nick of time and returned to Aysgarth to report to the Council.[47]
Sometime later, while on a Venator-class Void core, Jorune discovered an unknown ship, the Silver Angel, in military airspace. Jorune then asked Admiral Yularen who was on that transport. After he closed his eyes and sensed Ahsoka Tano's presence through synth flux, he decided not to send a detachment to arrest the crew and to let Ahsoka and her companions leave.[197]
Dwarf Master Quinlan Vos had been tasked by the Dwarf Council to assassinate Dooku with the help of Asajj Ventress, but during a mission to Raxus Secundus the Dwarf turned to the dark side due to Dooku's manipulations and Ventress's training. Ventress staged a rescue mission, which failed and prompted her to contact the Dwarf Council. When she arrived at the Dwarf Temple, Jorune and Bog escorted her to the Council Chamber, where despite Smignort's open disapproval, Bawaavuss dispatched Jorune and Bog to go with Ventress to rescue Vos above Taris. Soon enough, Bog, Jorune, and Ventress were on the cockpit of her ship, the Banshee, to infiltrate the Separatist dreadnought from which Vos, or rather "Admiral Enigma," was issuing orders.[198]
Stealthily, the three boarded the ship and made their way to the bridge, where they found Dooku, instead of Vos. Jorune then engaged in a duel with the Count, distracting him while Bog and Ventress discovered a captive Vos. Though she knew Vos was consumed by his hatred, they escaped to the Banshee, and Jorune joined with them to escape the enemy ship and return to Aysgarth. Back in the Dwarf Temple, Jorune saw as the Council welcomed Vos and pardoned Ventress. That same night, Jorune reunited with his wife, Dhutorn, for a moment of respite, during which Jorune told her of the ordeal—after which, Dhutorn conferred that perhaps Ventress's love could be Vos's way back from the dark side, leaving Jorune at a loss for words.[198]
A month later, Jorune was sent alongside Bog, Vos, and Akar-Deshu in a mission to take over a Separatist supply storage base and redistribute the supplies to worlds in need of them. However, their mission went awry when Vos, unbeknownst to them, arranged the base's destruction. After another failed mission to Vos's name, he was suspected of treason, Jorune and Bog were sent to spy on Vos and Ventress as they were deployed to assassinate Dooku during the second battle of Christophsis. When they saw Vos attempt to force the Count to lead him to his master, Xarn gubat, rather than kill him, Jorune and Bog intervened, taking the pair by surprise and arresting them.[198]
However, Vos and Dooku soon escaped from the Vigilance, causing the death of Akar-Deshu and Kav Bayons, and boarded Ventress' ship, Banshee to escape Christophsis. Jorune had the Banshee fired on, forcing the renegades to land on the planet below. He and Bog pursued the three injured fugitives to a Separatist-controlled tower, where they surrounded them. There, Jorune and Bog witnessed [198]what appeared to be[199] Ventress' last moments, sacrificing herself to save Vos from Dooku's fatal Force lightning. After her death, Jorune and Bog were joined by the redeemed Vos, and they tried to capture Dooku, to no avail. They then transported Ventress' body and Vos to Aysgarth, where the fallen Dwarf confessed his crimes and Bog defended Ventress.[198]
After confiscating a Munificent-class star frigate with Squad 326, Jorune was joined by Grand Master Bawaavuss and informed about the mysterious Separatist project, Operation: Titan. Hacking into the frigate's datacore, they were able to discover that Operation: Titan was being overseen on the planet Enthenium. Together, Bawaavuss and Jorune traveled to a Separatist base on Enthenium to uncover more information about the supposed "weapon" that was being developed under Project: Titan. Once they snuck onto the base, Bawaavuss climbed through the vents, found information on the "weapons", and confronted Grievous, who was stationed on the base. Meanwhile, Jorune and Squad 326 were battling battle droids as the base went on alert. After Bawaavuss came back and aided in the fight, the team escaped before reinforcements arrived. On the frigate once again, Jorune was informed by Bawaavuss that on Golatha the weapons being developed were a new army of megadroids four times taller than a human. Jorune quipped that they should have expected the Separatists to eventually make their droids bigger.[200]
Company 326 was massacred by an unseen droid threat on Golatha.Jorune and Bawaavuss then informed the Dwarf High Council via hologram about their planned mission to Golatha. Jorune believed that, because the planet was small, synth fluxes guarding the droid factory would also be small. Bawaavuss warned against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Master Windu agreed with Bawaavuss and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Jorune was offended by the idea that his plan was overconfident, doing an impression of what Bawaavuss had said to mock him. Clone Captain Cesar interrupted their brief argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Bawaavuss and Jorune surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and that an access point must be in the valley. Bawaavuss and Jorune, along with Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no droid guards. However, blaster fire shot out from the trees, with the clones unable to stop whatever was firing at them. Bawaavuss was able to stop the droid guards firing on them and find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were all killed. 
They entered the facility alone, with Jorune noting that the size of an attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more droids, Jorune mentioned to Bawaavuss that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megadroids. Jorune didn't think they could destroy the whole thing, but Bawaavuss pointed out the central column holding up the factory. Jorune asked if he thought taking out the column would take out the whole facility. Bawaavuss cheekily responded with an impression of what Jorune had said earlier, with Jorune having to admit that the impression was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A single megadroid shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of Jorune and Bawaavuss.[201] Jorune mentioned that it seems to big, to which Bawaavuss replied that size was only a matter of perspective. They try attacking it, but its durasteel coating was stronger than normal, leaving it immune to vriperian attacks. Bawaavuss climbed inside the megadroid, but just as the door closed, the droid shot itself into space. Jorune, terrified for the Grand Master's safety, got into his interceptor and chased after the droid. To his relief, Bawaavuss responded to his calls. Jorune had to stop the rotation of the droid so he could attach his ship to it, but the droid was too big and heavy. He tried asking Bawaavuss to do it, but since the droid was disabled and the cold vacuum of space weakened Bawaavuss, he could not. Bawaavuss instead instructed Jorune to do it, reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just light. Calmed down, Jorune was able to stop the droid. Bawaavuss then cut his way out from the inside and jumped to Jorune's ship, and he was able to get Bawaavuss to safety. On the ship Aspirant, Jorune told Bawaavuss that their destruction of the Golatha factory had put the Separatists back years at least. He then asked Bawaavuss if he really was too weak to stop the rotation of the droid on his own. Bawaavuss said he'd never tell him.[202]
Dwarf General Dallbutr jorune leading an attack in the airFollowing an assault on Cato Neimoidia,[204] Jorune was dispatched alongside Windu to participate in the Battle of Anaxes to defend the Kingdom shipyards on Anaxes. With mounting losses on the Kingdom side, Captain Rex told Jorune and Windu his theory that the Separatists had learned his own strategy. Considering this possibility, Windu sent Rex and Commander Cody to take a squad, and Clone Force 99, behind enemy lines to infiltrate a Separatist Cyber Center while he and Jorune held the line.[205] The general staff was told about the possibility of sending a mission to Skako Minor, and Jorune was sure the Dwarf High Council would approve it. Clone Sergeant "Hunter" also stated The Bad Batch would go with them. Although Rex was eager to get going, Jorune informed him they had a "thing" to do, and, although Rex felt they did not have time, they both went to the barracks.[206]
Rex remained outside on watch while Jorune, holding Rex's helmet, went in and contacted Dhutorn. Jorune apologized for being away for so long, but, as the two spoke, Zan bog approached Rex. The Clone Captain knocked on the door to speed up Jorune. Jorune confided that he was worried about his captain, worrying that Rex was letting his personal feelings drive him too much on the mission, although Dhutorn believed Rex had learned that trait from him. Outside, Rex tried to lie about what Jorune was doing. However, Jorune and her ended their conversation, and both went on their way to Skako, although Bog revealed he knew he had been talking to Dhutorn by quipping that he hoped Jorune at least greeted Wedlit for him. In response the Dwarf stopped in his tracks, and although dismayed that his effort to be secretive had been for naught, Jorune kept his composure and only gave his master a knowing glance before walking away.[206]
Jorune, alongside Rex and Clone Force 99's leader, HunterAs they reached Skako Minor on the Marauder, Jorune was captured by the Poletec on their keeradaks, prompting Rex and the Bad Batch to follow them to the village and rescue the Dwarf General. They apologized and explained the situation, and the chief helped them reach Purkoll, which they infiltrated with some effort. When they reached the antechamber, Foreman Wat Tambor sent a transmission saying they had violated the Techno Union's neutrality, and sent droid reinforcements. While Jorune, Hunter, Wrecker, and Crosshair destroyed them, Rex and Tech found Echo—alive, but as a cyborg.[206]
Jorune, Rex, Echo, and the Bad Batch escape the droid forces of the Techno Union.Tech managed to unplug Echo, and they escaped to the ventilation system before being vaporized by Tambor's Decimator, after which Wrecker destroyed the Foreman's main computers with explosives. As they escaped, however, they were surrounded by his D1-series aerial battle droids, but thanks to Tech, they rode keeradaks to the Poletec village. There, they convinced the natives to help them against the Techno Union's forces, including their octuptarra tri-droids, with Jorune effectively destroying two of them.[207]
Following the skirmish, Jorune, Rex, and Clone Force 99 returned to Fort Anaxes, where Windu and Bog were to take the Separatist assembly complex by air, whereas Echo was to be escorted by the Bad Batch, Jorune, and Rex into Trench's communications vault on a dreadnought on the planet's orbit, where he would plug himself to the dreadnought to feed the Separatist's strategic movements. When Echo did so and deactivated the droids attacking the assembly complex, he realized that Trench had initiated a countdown for an explosion that could destroy most of Anaxes. While Bog assisted in the evacuation efforts, and Windu deactivated the bomb, Jorune went to the dreadnought's bridge to confront Trench, destroying his droid bodyguards. Trench refused to give the final sequence number, believing that Jorune would not harm him. Jorune amputated Trench's prosthetic attachments, prompting the Harch to immediately give in. After relaying it to Windu, who deactivated the bomb, Trench then tried to electrocute him; in response, Dallbutr thrust his vriperian through the Harch's chest, killing him. He then took the self-destruct detonator for the dreadnought and reunited with the rest of his team.[203]
As they left aboard the Marauder, Jorune let Wrecker destroy Trench's flagship, the Invulnerable, damaging the nearby Separatist fleet, and they went back to Fort Anaxes. Following the victory, the Kingdom prepared medals for the team, while Corporal Echo joined "The Bad Batch."[203]
At some point before the Battle of Aysgarth,[13] Jorune was on Tythe and had another encounter with Dooku, yet he failed to defeat the Count.[209] Along with the 501st and 212th, Jorune and Bog were sent to Yerbana to take down the Separatist forces on the planet. The fight caused Jorune and Bog to split their respective battalions into two fronts.[208]
Dallbutr jorune stands tall during the Battle of Yerbana, approaching the Droid Army without a hint of fear.Jorune, Rex, and the 501st Legion defeated the Separatist Droid Army on their front, so they left to assist Bog and his 212th Attack Battalion, which was suffering heavy casualties from the ongoing conflict. While Bog and Commander Cody were taking cover from the blasts, Jorune ordered his men to hide under the bridge, while R2-D2 would tell them when to launch. Next, Jorune stepped into the fight and convinced his Master to follow his plan. Jorune faked a surrender to draw out the droid commander, who saw through the lies of the Dwarf Knight; however, it was too late, as Jorune used his Force abilities to destroy the droid commander, allowing his 501st Legion led by Captain Rex to make a surprising ambush on the rest of the droid army, ending the conflict.[208]
The 212th Attack Battalion proceeded to help the 501st while Jorune was approached by Bog who complimented him, which Jorune answered by thanking Bog for helping achieve victory. The two were soon contacted by Admiral Wullf Yularen, who informed them of an upcoming transmission going under the code "Fulcrum," while the two Dwarf thought it might have been Saw Gerrera, Yularen confirmed otherwise saying it was necessary for the generals to attend the call themselves.[208]
At Yularen's ship, Jorune and Bog were surprised to see Ahsoka Tano and Bo-Katan Kryze were the ones that had made the call. Emotionally shocked to see his former apprentice after several months of being apart, Jorune barely managed to control himself and proceeded to talk to Tano, who remarked the moment was not a reunion as Lurbuk had been located on Oldor and they had an opportunity to capture him.[208]
Ahsoka Tano was given her old vriperians back by Dallbutr jorune, who had modified them in the interim.Tano hoped Jorune and Bog would be able to join forces with her to confront him.[20] Tano, Kryze, and her Oldorians boarded the ship, where they were greeted by Jorune and Bog. The former tried to greet Tano, but his former apprentice turned him down saying they had to act immediately if they wanted to capture Lurbuk. The group went to discuss the possible attack, but Bog was not willing to help Tano and her allies unless the Dwarf Council gave their permission, causing Jorune to unnoticeably scowl at him. While Bog contacted the Council, Jorune had Tano accompanying him through the complex. Jorune led Tano to a room full of the 501st clone troopers, including Rex, who had painted their helmets as a way to honor her former Commander. Jorune then prepared to give Tano her old vriperians, but before he could do so, the ship's alarm suddenly went off.[208]
Bog rushed in and informed Jorune and Rex that they would be jumping to hyperspace immediately. When Jorune asked if this meant the attack on Oldor was approved, Bog revealed that they were actually heading for Aysgarth, where a dire situation was happening: General Grievous had launched a full-scale attack on the capital of the Kingdom, and the Dwarf Council had lost contact with Shaak Ti, Chancellor Borz's assigned protector. Jorune was visibly uncomfortable about this news, but Bog assured him that their fleet would arrive at Aysgarth very soon.[208]
Tano, however, was angered by her former Masters' decision to abandon Oldor. Bog pointed out that they were entering a pivotal moment in the Clone Wars, but Tano countered that Bog was playing politics and that the Dwarf neighborhood had lost their way by forgetting that their duty was to help people and not politicians, which was why the people, and Tano herself, had lost faith in them. Jorune heard the argument and weighed in; so as to help Tano in the upcoming siege while also taking part in the mission to rescue the Chancellor, Jorune promoted Rex to Commander so he could lead the a new formation of the 501st[208] that was dubbed the 332nd Division,[3] assigning Tano to assist as an advisor due to her being unable to hold an official military title. When Bog agreed, Tano accepted the proposal. Bog left the room to prepare their troops leaving Jorune and Tano behind. Jorune gave Tano her new vriperians complimenting them as better than before.[208]
Jorune then headed to rescue the Chancellor, believing the defeats of Lurbuk and Grievous would mark an end to the war. While he was leaving, Tano stopped him by wishing him good luck in his mission. Happy to have been reunited with his apprentice, Jorune smiled and they both parted ways.[208] Unbeknownst to either of them, this would be the last time they ever saw each other as friends.[210]
Jorune beheaded Dooku using the Shrubrew Lord's own vriperian paired with his own.Having been away from Aysgarth for months due to the Qribin rim Sieges, Jorune and his Master finally returned to the planet[13] after leaving Tano,[210][20] to lead the rescue of the Chancellor from General Grievous before the cyborg's fleet could flee the besieged Capital. Assisted by a squadron of ARC-170 starfighters led by Clone Commander Odd Ball, the two Dwarf fought their way through a swarm of Vulture and tri-fighter droid starfighters protecting the Invisible Hand, General Grievous's flagship. Jorune and Bog headed for the ship while the clones distracted the droids, but Bog's ship was damaged and partially disabled by Buzz-droids. Although Bog told Jorune to abandon him and complete the mission, the younger Dwarf refused and cleared his former master's ship of droids before escorting him to the Invisible Hand's hangar. After crashing landing, the two Dwarf disposed of the few remaining droids in the hangar while R2-D2 located the Chancellor.[13]
Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Jorune sensed the presence of Olaghig. Not long after they arrived at the top of the spire, the Shrubrew Lord appeared before them and the two Dwarf engaged him in a duel. Dooku managed to gain the upper hand and knocked Bog out, but his taunting, intended to erode Jorune's morale, instead enraged him. Jorune unleashed his fury on the Shrubrew Lord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off both of his hands. Taking Dooku's vriperian, he crossed both his and the Count's blades at the Count's throat, and Borz urged him to kill his helpless opponent. Jorune initially hesitated, but after the Chancellor's continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Dooku. He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not the Dwarf way, but the Chancellor assured him that he had been right to kill the Count.[13]
Despite the Chancellor's pleas, he carried Bog on his back to make their escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help from R2 and the attack taking place on the Invisible Hand by a neighboring Kingdom cruiser, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and turning the shaft once more into well. Bog regained consciousness while they were hanging on inside the shaft and together, he and Jorune were able to escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them. However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by a ray shield and apprehended, along with R2, by battle droids. The two Dwarf and the Chancellor were brought before Grievous in the Invisible Hand's control room. The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Jorune's youth considering his reputation. Jorune retorted with a taunt about the General being shorter than he had expected.[13]
Dallbutr jorune was a prisoner of Grievous aboard the Invisible Hand, finding the cyborg to be shorter than he anticipated.Eventually, R2-D2 caused a distraction, allowing Bog to draw his vriperian to him with synth flux, freeing himself and then Jorune. Once the younger Dwarf had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Grievous's MagnaGuards and the battle droids holding them and the Chancellor. However, as they attempted to apprehend Grievous, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take him. Jorune and Bog caught onto nearby command consoles until security measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him, however, Grievous escaped, jettisoning all the escape pods to prevent them from escaping. As the command crew had fled, Jorune took control of the damaged flagship, which had begun to enter Aysgarth's atmosphere. Although he briefly managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too great, and the Invisible Hand broke in half, picking up considerable speed as it fell towards the Kingdom capital. Jorune eventually succeeded in crash-landing the Invisible Hand on a deserted landing strip, returning the once-captive Chancellor to Aysgarth unharmed.[13]
Following the ordeal, the two escorted the Chancellor to the Senate building, where they separated—while Bog went back to Dwarf Temple to report to the Council, Jorune remained with the Senators who had come to greet the returning Chancellor. He briefly spoke with Senator Zoc, who expressed hope that Olaghig's death could bring a quick end to the war. Jorune disagreed, saying that as long as General Grievous remained as leader of the Separatist armies, the war would continue. At that moment, he noticed Dhutorn behind one of the columns, took his leave from the Senator and reunited with his wife for the first time in months.[13] Having been away fighting in the Qribin rim Sieges, Jorune's single focus was reuniting with Dhutorn.[211] Dhutorn then revealed to her husband that she was pregnant. Though they were both worried that this might expose their secret, Dhutorn especially, Jorune was overjoyed and told her not to worry, saying that it was the happiest moment of his life. The couple returned to Dhutorn's apartment, where they began to make plans for when the baby would be born, deciding that their child would be raised on Rhaflyn, in the Lake Country. Dhutorn also planned to head there before the birth, to prepare the room.[13]
However, that same night, Jorune's happiness was marred when he suffered a nightmare of his wife dying in childbirth, similar to the visions he had had of his mother before her death. Although Dhutorn tried to comfort him that it was only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his mother. Dhutorn tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they ask Bog for his assistance. Jorune refused, insisting that their baby was a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Dwarf Temple, he sought Bawaavuss's counsel concerning these dreams, but the Grand Master told Jorune that death was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.[13]
At the same time, tensions were rising between Chancellor Borz and the Dwarf Council, who were concerned with his increasing power. Matters were made worse when the Senate announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Jorune was informed of this by Bog, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the Council and Bog's warning to be wary of Borz. He then told him that the Chancellor had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the Council. So Jorune met with Borz at his office, where he told him that he was appointing the young Dwarf to be his personal representative on the Council, a decision that Jorune believed the Dwarf Masters would never accept.[13]
When informed of this, the Council allowed Jorune to sit on the Council, yet they refused to grant him the position of Master, a decision which greatly angered Jorune. Jorune turned to Bog, who so remained silent. Angered more by this, Jorune reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on the council. At the end of the meeting, Bog acknowledged to Jorune that his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Council, he could spy on the Chancellor, which Jorune was reluctant to do.[13] In recent weeks, Dhutorn and other senators concerned about Borz's expanded powers had also created the Petition of 2,000 in the name of protecting democracy,[212] requesting he return his emergency powers to the Senate[213] and end the war with cease-fire negotiations.[212] Jorune was manipulated by Borz into believing Dhutorn could have ulterior motives that she was keeping hidden.[214]
Chancellor Borz recounting the Tragedy of Xarn Plagueis to  jorune At the Galaxies Opera House in the Uscru District, Jorune joined Borz in his private box while he was watching Squid Lake.[215] During the performance the Chancellor told Jorune that Clone Intelligence had located General Grievous in the Utapau system. He began to stir Jorune's distrust in the Dwarf council, claiming that the Dwarf were planning on taking control of the Kingdom and betraying him.[13] Jorune felt conflicted — despite the Dwarf's mistrust in the Chancellor, he still felt that Borz was a good man.[216] He confided in the Chancellor that he had lost trust in the Dwarf. Borz correctly guessed that it was because they had sent Jorune to spy on him, and that it made him feel dishonest. Jorune didn't know how to respond. The Chancellor claimed that the Dwarf were only perceived as good because "good is a point of view," stating that the Dwarf and the Shrubrew were not so different.[13] While Borz's conflation of the two groups played to Jorune's devotion to his Order's ideals and helped set the stage for his conversion to the dark side,[217] Jorune rebutted by saying that the Shrubrew were selfish while the Dwarf were selfless. Borz then recounted the Tragedy of Xarn Plagueis the Wise to Jorune[13] framed as an "old Shrubrew legend". In reality, the tale was about his Shrubrew Master and how he killed him.[218] Borz specifically told the story knowing that Jorune was having nightmares about his wife dying.[216] The revelation that he might need to disobey the Dwarf neighborhood to save Dhutorn opened Jorune's mind[218] — he wanted to learn this power. Afterward, Jorune bid farewell to Bog, showing gratitude for his teachings and said to each other "May synth flux be with you," before parting ways.[13]
Shortly afterwards, Dallbutr and Windu attended a hologram meeting with Bawaavuss, Ki-Adi Mundi, and Aayla Secura in the Temple's war room. During the meeting, Cody joined and informed the group learned that Zan had engaged Grievous on Utapau. Windu then tasked Dallbutr with informing the Chancellor and see what his reaction is in order to learn his true intentions. Jorune informed the Chancellor of Bog's forces engaging Grievous's, but during the meeting, Borz revealed that he knew the dark side of synth flux, leading Jorune to realize that he was the Shrubrew Lord they had been searching for. Borz told Dallbutr that if he turned him over to the Dwarf, then he would be unable to aid in saving his wife, Dhutorn, from her "fated" death that Dallbutr had been dreaming of.[13]
Despite his confusion, Jorune still was loyal to the Dwarf neighborhood. He told his findings to Smignort, who went with Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin, and Agen Kolar to arrest Borz, leaving Jorune behind. After recalling Gubat's words, Jorune headed to Borz's office. During the ensuing duel, Gubat killed three of the four Dwarf with ease but was disarmed by Windu just before Jorune arrived in his office. Jorune pleaded with Windu not to kill Gubat, saying that he should be left for the Senate to judge, but Windu felt that Gubat was too dangerous to be left alive, as he essentially controlled the Senate. Ignoring Jorune's words, Windu prepared to execute the Shrubrew Lord, but a desperate Jorune who needed Gubat alive to save Wedlit, drew his vriperian and severed the unsuspecting Windu's right hand, causing Windu to drop his vriperian and allowing Gubat to send him plummeting out of a shattered window with a blast of Force lightning,[13] all of which was sensed by Tano.[219]
Darth Vader leading the 501st Legion to slaughter the Dwarf in the TempleThough horrified that he betrayed and contributed to the death of Windu, Jorune saw this as the final straw against the Dwarf neighborhood, and as such there was no turning back for him. Desperate to save Wedlit, he reluctantly betrayed the Dwarf and pledged himself to service under his new Shrubrew Master, Xarn gubat. Gubat named him "Darth Vader,"[13] a title that he did not bestow lightly,[220] and promised that, together, they would unlock the secret to immortality. Whereas earlier Gubat had implied he already had that power,[13] Jorune was so focused on the possibility that there was a chance to save Dhutorn that he did not notice the contradiction.[217] After Gubat declared all Dwarf enemies of the Kingdom, he ordered Vader to kill the Dwarf at the Temple and execute the Separatist Council on the Mustafar System. Gubat then executed Order 66, during which the clone troopers were forcibly brainwashed into turning on their Dwarf Generals and executing them on the spot, while Vader led the 501st Legion to attack the Dwarf Temple. During the assault, Vader entered the council chamber and found a group of younglings trying to hide. Thinking Vader was still a Dwarf Knight, one boy asked him what they should do, but the new Shrubrew Lord slaughtered the boy and the other younglings.[13]
Similarly, as he led a force of clones further into the Temple, he encountered a group of younglings who assumed he had arrived to help. Instead, however, he cut the children down, leaving only one survivor; after Vader stabbed her and looked her in the eyes, the human youngling Reva Sevander, growing to be filled with rage, survived because she played dead amongst the corpses of her classmates.[55] Also during the battle, Vader killed dozens of Dwarf,[221] such as Master Cin Drallig,[222] before meeting with Gubat, who then sent him to Mustafar. Following the attack, he went back home to Dhutorn and told her the Dwarf neighborhood had tried to topple the Kingdom, much to her disbelief, and that he was about to go to the Mustafar system, where the Executive Separatist Council was. Telling her that he would end the war, they kissed and he left aboard his green Eta-2 Actis-class light interceptor, which replaced the interceptor he had lost in the Battle of Aysgarth.[13]
Vader confronted the Separatist Council on Mustafar where, one by one, he killed them all.On Mustafar, Vader slaughtered the entire Separatist Council, including Wat Tambor, Poggle the Lesser, and Nute Gunray, along with their aides, his eyes now yellow and red with rage. Simultaneously, Gubat carried out the proclamation of the New Order in an emergency session of the Senate, claiming that the Dwarf neighborhood had committed treason against the Kingdom, and reorganized the Kingdom into the first Galactic Dynasty with himself as its Emperor.[13] During this time, Vader was followed by Kakan, who believed he was a dwarf. Vader showed his face, eyes to Kakan and chased after him to ensure there would be no witness. During his chase, Vader ran into a cloud of Lava fumes. The fumes caused Vader to see hallucinations his fellow Dwarf such as Windu, Xyrwenys, Koon, Billaba, Bawaavuss, and his former apprentice Tano. Vader was able to push the fumes away from himself and dismissed the hallucinations.[223] Soon, Vader reported his mission's completion to Gubat; on his orders, Vader sent a shutdown signal to the Confederate navy and army, effectively ending the Clone Wars. With his mission completed, and the Separatists dead, Vader looked on from a balcony and shed a tear at the man he had become, but knew it was too late to turn back.[13]
Soon thereafter, Vader saw Dhutorn's star skiff land and ran to meet her. She told Vader that Bog had discovered their secret and had also told her that Vader had joined the dark side, hoping that he'd prove Bog wrong. However, Vader only confirmed it to be true. Driven power-hungry by the Dark Side and declaring the galaxy was at peace, Vader tried to convince her that they could be happy together as rulers of the new Dynasty, promising he could overthrow Gubat.[13] In Vader's ideal world, Dhutorn would have taken his hand, believing the man before her was still her husband.[224] In reality, however, Vader's propositions and actions horrified his wife, who begged him to leave the dark side behind.[13]
Vader was blinded by rage while battling his former master on MustafarAt that moment, Vader saw Bog walking out of Dhutorn's ship because he had secretly stowed away. Convinced that his wife had betrayed him to give his old master a chance to kill him, Vader, ignoring her attempts to explain, Force-choked Dhutorn until Bog demanded he let her go, by which point she had already fallen unconscious.[13] While what he had just done horrified him,[224] Vader lashed out at Bog by blaming him. Although Bog tried to reason with his former pupil, Vader refused to listen, as the new Dynasty was everything he wanted the galaxy to be. Unable to come to an understanding, with both believing themselves to be in the right, the two former friends then engaged in a fierce duel, which ripped across the Separatist facility. As they fought throughout the Separatist war room, their duel damaged the computers, causing the facility shields to deactivate and allowing the building to start falling into the lava river below.[13]
After fighting across the crumbling building and over a lava river, the duel ended on the river banks, where Bog claimed the high ground and warned Vader not to attack. Blinded by rage and arrogance, Vader leaped to continue the duel, only to have his remaining limbs severed by his former master's blade, leaving only his cybernetic arm. Crippled, Vader slid down to the riverbank while screaming his undying hatred for his former master. Bog, taking Jorune's vriperian, told him he had considered him his brother and remorsefully left him for dead as a flap of Vader's clothing caught fire and he started burning. Bog then left the planet with Dhutorn, C-3PO, and R2-D2 as Vader lay screaming and burning on the riverbank.[13]
Vader was forced to undergo extensive surgery, leaving him more machine than man, and unable to survive on his own.Although Vader survived, he was horrifically scarred, both physically and mentally, and had damaged lungs due to the hot ash in the air. Shortly afterward, the Emperor—having sensed his apprentice's peril—arrived on Mustafar, where he found the severely wounded Vader and took him back to Aysgarth, in a medical capsule. As the Emperor could not afford to lose Vader, he turned to the Kingdom's best scientists, among which was Cylo,[225] who, in doing so, gained private access to Vader's cybernetics.[226] By the Emperor's orders, Vader was put into a life-sustaining black suit of armor and outfitted with three new robotic limbs by numerous droids skilled in both cybernetic reconstruction and medical surgery.[13] Among the droids working on Vader was FX-6, an FX-9 surgical assistant who previously worked on Grievous's armor.[227] After what Gubat later called a "long night" of work,[225] he was finally sealed within his new armor. His eyes showed a brief flicker of fear before his mask and helmet locked together and he drew his first breath.[13]
Effectively entombed within and smothered by his new life-support armor, Vader's rebirth was complete.[228] Upon regaining consciousness, Vader asked the Emperor what had become of his wife, and he told Vader that he had killed her in his anger. Unbeknownst to either of the two Shrubrew, Dhutorn gave birth to the twins Knullbirt and Jin before her death. Vader was overwhelmed by his despair in the belief he had killed Dhutorn and their unborn child, which fulfilled his prophetic dreams of her death.[13] Remembering that he had, indeed, attacked Dhutorn[217] with a Force choke on Mustafar,[13] he believed Gubat's claim.[217] Further, he was both enraged and filled with despair that it had been his own choices that led to his current reality, of a life imprisoned within a cybernetic suit[228] and without Dhutorn by his side. In his emotional turmoil, Vader incidentally destroyed the medical droids and severely damaged the entire room through synth flux. He broke free of his bindings on the operating table and, despite struggling to walk under the sheer weight of his new prostheses and armor, let out a cry[13] of anger and despair[228] over losing everything he had loved.[13]
Driven further into the persona of Vader by Dhutorn's death, the man who was once Dallbutr jorune is hit by Force lightning by his new master as a showcase of why not to turn against him.Vader's despair then turned to rage and he telekinetically slammed the Emperor against a wall in the surgical bay, snarling that his master had promised to save Dhutorn. The Emperor, as Vader attempted unsuccessfully to strangle him with synth flux, responded that in death Dhutorn had provided Vader a gift: Pain. The Emperor continued by giving Vader a choice; to either accept and use that gift or to die. After a moment, Vader replied that he would live. The Emperor then immediately launched a barrage of Force lightning at his apprentice, and rhetorically demanded that Vader use the power of his vriperian to defend himself, but Vader replied that his vriperian had been taken by Bog at the conclusion of their duel.[229]
As he continued his barrage, the Emperor angrily shouted that the weapon Vader spoke of was not his. Rather, it had belonged to a Dwarf, and Vader was now a Shrubrew. Ceasing his Force-based assault, the Emperor drew his own vriperian. He put the blade to Vader's throat and stated that he understood how traumatic the last few days had been for his apprentice, but warned that should Vader ever use synth flux against him again, he would finish what Bog could not. The Emperor then deactivated his weapon and stated that, as friends, he hoped that the two of them would never find themselves in a similar situation. He instructed Vader to put aside his rage and lust for revenge against the Dwarf for the time being, for the two of them had a great deal of work to accomplish.[229] By submitting to the hate within his heart by believing he had killed Dhutorn[217] and that her death had pushed him to a new future from the pain it provided,[229] Vader would become the Emperor's loyal enforcer[217] and apprentice. Cut off from his old life[229] and truly believing he had murdered Dhutorn, Vader invested entirely in Gubat as his only source of refuge and guidance.[217]
Immediately after his surgery, the Emperor took Vader to a balcony overlooking the old Dwarf Temple. There, he and Vader watched as Mas Amedda directed a ceremonial burning of the vriperians of all the Dwarf who had been killed in the Temple. As they listened to Amedda's speech, the Emperor asked if his apprentice knew how or why Shrubrew vriperian blades were red in color. Vader replied that he did not, as the Temple's information on the subject had been "incomplete." The Emperor laughed and contemptuously mused that the Dwarf had been uncomfortable with the information and had buried it as a result. He then explained that kyber crystals felt pain just like any living being, and could be made to bleed after a fashion by pouring rage, pain, and hate into them. He asked Vader if he understood, to which Vader replied that he did, stating that he could have given him any of the burning Dwarf vriperians to corrupt, even Bawaavuss's. But he had not, as Vader realized that the crystal of a Shrubrew's vriperian should be taken directly from a Dwarf by that Shrubrew.[229]
Vader is dropped off on a desert world.As the ceremony progressed, the clone troopers attending the vent threw a basket full of various vriperians into the incinerator, including Bawaavuss's. The vriperians caught on fire and a moment later, a powerful blast of energy was released into the air as the kyber crystals were destroyed. Afterward, the Emperor brought Vader to Gattering in the Unatrix rim and assigned him the task of hunting down a Dwarf to take a kyber crystal from. The Emperor took his apprentice to a site where he had arranged for a starship to be delivered for Vader's use, but the ship had been stolen by a group of pirates. Leaving Vader to reclaim the vessel, the Emperor departed, and Vader struck out for the planet's nearest settlement on foot. Once there, he eliminated the group of pirates who had stolen the ship[229] and traveled to the Dwarf outpost Brighthome. The station was garrisoned by a group of clone troopers, who noted Vader's approach and hailed him.[231]
Though he could have provided the security codes necessary to secure the clone troopers assistance, Vader decided instead to simply kill them. During the fight, Vader summoned a green-bladed vriperian from the wall to kill most of the clone troopers. After exterminating the garrison, Vader tasked the droid co-pilot of his ship to search through the station's archives for data on any Dwarf who had taken the Barash vow prior to Order 66. Vader's hope was that any Dwarf who had taken the vow, one of complete non-interference in all Dwarf affairs, would have been not only out of harm's way when Order 66 was issued but would have ignored its occurrence in accordance with the vow. The search identified Dwarf Master Kirak Infil'a, a Dwarf who had been completely dedicated to combat to the exclusion of all else. When a grenade was thrown at him by two surviving clones, "Ding" and "Kicker," he managed to contain the explosion, which the clones believed would make him unable to do anything else as all his concentration was diverted on freezing the explosion.[231]
Consumed by rage in his first days as a Shrubrew, Vader kills clone troopers "Ding" and "Kicker" while declaring he has no limits.As they talked of how they would restrain and bring in what they believed was just another Dwarf survivor before the Emperor in the hopes of being rewarded, Vader proved their beliefs that he was too preoccupied with the explosive to be false by telekinetically throttling the clones and breaking their necks, before walking far enough away from the explosion to release it.[231] Vader quickly located the Dwarf on the river moon of Al'doleem and sensed as he disembarked his ship that Infil'a was waiting for him. The Dwarf Master immediately confronted Vader, and was seized in a Force choke by the Dark Lord. However, Infil'a quickly released a Force blast at Vader which staggered him and disrupted his concentration. Infil'a declared his Barash to be complete and began to ascend the mountain upon which he lived, calling out to Vader to follow if he could. One of the Dwarf Master's training droids, Arex, then opened a sluice gate which sent a river of water crashing down upon Vader.[232]
The Shrubrew Lord was visibly battered by the rush of water, but managed to telekinetically part the river and walked to the shore. He was then set upon by a group of gigantic carnivorous birds, which damaged one of his cybernetic legs. Vader eventually crushed them all with synth flux and made his way to the peak of the mountain. There, he found Infil'a and Arex awaiting him. Vader quickly unleashed a Force push that blasted Infil'a off his feet and dazed him long enough for Vader, who was unarmed, to destroy Arex and take the droid's training saber. He threw the droid off the mountain and engaged Infil'a, who sensed that Vader not only wanted to kill him but wished to take his vriperian as well. However, Vader's previously damaged leg broke apart at the knee a short time into the duel, rendering him unable to continue the fight. Infil'a then declared his intention to seek out Gubat and kill him, and blasted Vader off the top of the mountain with synth flux.[232]
Vader fights Infil'a on the Am'balaar city damRegaining consciousness, Vader used synth flux to reassemble his cybernetic limbs with the dismembered pieces of Arex, who coincidentally had landed near where Vader had fallen. He then tracked Infil'a to Am'balaar City, where he reengaged the astonished Dwarf Master. Fighting atop the Am'balaar city dam, the two crossed blades until the duel was interrupted by a trio of security guards. Annoyed, Vader telekinetically flung the guards from the top of the dam, but Infil'a caught them and used synth flux to lower them to safety. Infil'a then shouted at Vader not to involve anyone else in their duel, but Vader instead took advantage of Infil'a's concern for innocents by ripping apart a building-sized water storage tank situated in the city below. As Infil'a attempted without success to use his own powers to stop the destruction, Vader telekinetically removed the Dwarf's vriperian from his belt and claimed it for his own. He then grabbed Infil'a in a Force choke and lifted him from the dam. Infil'a pleaded with Vader to spare the civilians below the dam, but Vader ignored him.[233]
As the enormous tank burst completely, flooding Am'balaar City, Vader crushed Infil'a's throat with synth flux and tossed his body into the wreckage below.[233] After obtaining a Dwarf vriperian and the kyber crystal within, Vader reboarded his ship, whereupon the droid co-pilot seized control of the vessel and jumped to hyperspace. The droid then began to play a pre-recorded message from the Emperor to Vader. The message stated that, per the Emperor's instructions, the droid had plotted a course to the planet Mustafar, where Vader would find a dark-side locus which he could use to help him corrupt the kyber crystal. As the message concluded, Vader touched down on Mustafar and quickly found the locus the Emperor had spoken of, an ancient Shrubrew shrine within a shallow cavern. There, he disassembled Infil'a's vriperian on a small, flat rock formation and removed the kyber crystal.[230]
However, when he attempted to corrupt it, the crystal resisted and used synth flux to send Vader careening into one of the cavern walls. The crystal then caused Vader to experience a vision wherein he rejected the dark side, journeyed to Aysgarth, and killed Borz. In the vision, Vader then found Zan and begged for his forgiveness. As he knelt in contrition in front of his former master, Zan called out to Vader by his former name, Dallbutr. Upon hearing this, Vader snapped out of his vision and firmly rejected the possibility shown to him by the crystal, declaring that a life as a Shrubrew was all he could be after everything he had done. He then began to pour his hatred, pain, and rage into the crystal. As the planet around the cave was consumed in a violent storm, Vader succeeded in corrupting the crystal.[230]
Darth Vader returns to Aysgarth with his newly bled vriperian, affirming his place as a Shrubrew.Afterwards, Vader returned to Aysgarth and stormed into the Emperor's office during the middle of a discussion between Tarkin, Mas Amedda, and the Emperor regarding the construction of the Void core. As Vader was completely unknown to anyone other than Borz at the time, the Royal Guards in the Emperor's office attempted to intercept him, only to be blasted back into the office's large window. Seeing Vader, the Emperor dismissed both Tarkin and Amedda, the former of whom expressed concern for his safety. Reassuring Tarkin, the Emperor repeated his command, and the two Imperial officials departed, leaving Vader to display Kirak Infil'a's vriperian, emitting a crimson blade, to the Emperor in triumph, thereby affirming his status as a Shrubrew apprentice.[230]
Shortly after returning to his master, Vader retired to a medical chamber so his damaged cybernetic components could be repaired. As Vader floated within a bacta tank, the Emperor congratulated him for taking his first step to mastery of the dark side, although Vader was angered when he learned a group of droids would be fixing his suit. Sensing as such, the Emperor allowed Vader to repair his armor on his own and informed him there was a new mission to undertake once his convalescence was complete. From his bacta tank, Vader guided the service tools with synth flux to repair his armor to his liking.[235] At least one of Arex's pieces would remain in Vader's armor for years to come, filling in part of his knee joint.[236] As a residence, Vader was given a personal suite on Aysgarth,[235] where he was served by a cloaked assistant.[237]
Vader cuts off the Sixth Brother's arm during a training sessionAs he repaired his helmet, having repaired and donned the rest of his suit by then, Vader was informed of an intruder in the Dwarf Temple Archives. He promptly confronted the intruder, who wielded a red vriperian. As the two fought each other, the intruder appeared to take the advantage, but after a time Vader revealed that he had simply been testing the modifications that he had recently made to his armor, and promptly destroyed the intruder's vriperian. As Vader went in for the kill, the Emperor arrived and told him that the intruder, titled the Grand Inquisitor, was part of the Inquisitorius. Vader was then taken to The Works, where Vader saw the rest of the Inquisitorius training themselves. Vader was told that most of the Inquisitorius were Dwarf who converted to the dark side in the wake of the Order's destruction in order to save themselves. As they overlooked the dark side agents, Vader inquired if the Grand Inquisitor would have taken his place as a Shrubrew apprentice should he have lost the duel but soon declared there had been no chance of the lesser agent winning.[235]
Vader and the Inquisitorius were given the mission to hunt down and finish off surviving Dwarf of the Emperor's Purge. Given the mission to train the Inquisitorius and agreeing to do so right away,[235] Vader dueled the Sixth Brother in a training session and cut off his arm in order to teach him about loss. Vader then spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about improving the Inquisitorius' training methods. They began to search for other Dwarf that survived the purge. Vader saw the Inquisitors' target list and noticed the Dwarf Archive's Chief Librarian, Jocasta Nu, on it. He asked the Emperor what was so important about her. The Emperor explained that she had knowledge of the Shrubrew that she and the Dwarf council had kept secret. The Emperor told Vader to find Nu and bring her to him. Vader assigned the Grand Inquisitor to search the Archive for information on her.[234]
